    State.LastFire = os.clock()

    -- Rivals holds no Tool for its weapons, so Activate() has nothing to call.
    -- The shot has to leave as a real click in the game's own input path.
    Util.Click()
end

--==================================================
-- HITBOX EXPANDER
--==================================================

local Hitbox = {}

-- Rivals resolves damage against its own hitbox rig, not against the cosmetic
-- MeshParts, so the expander writes to those parts and nothing else. The old
-- version welded a new box next to the rig, which the game never looked at.
local HITBOX_NAMES = {
    Head = { "HitboxHead", "HitboxHeadSmall", "PhysicalHitboxHead" },
    Torso = { "HitboxBody", "HitboxBodySmall" },
    Root = { "HitboxBody", "HitboxBodySmall" },
}

function Hitbox.Names(anchor)
    return HITBOX_NAMES[anchor] or HITBOX_NAMES.Head
end

function Hitbox.Remove(entry)
    -- Accepts either a cache entry or a bare Player. The type test is not
    -- cosmetic: indexing a Player instance with ".Player" raises "Player is not
    -- a valid member of Player", which killed the whole restore pass and left
    -- every hitbox expanded after the toggle dropped.
    local player = entry
    if type(entry) == "table" then
        player = entry.Player
    end
    if not player then
        return
    end
    local record = State.Hitboxes[player]
    if not record then
        return
    end

    for _, saved in ipairs(record) do
        pcall(function()
            saved.Part.Size = saved.Size
            saved.Part.Transparency = saved.Transparency
            saved.Part.Color = saved.Color
            saved.Part.CanCollide = saved.CanCollide
        end)
    end

    State.Hitboxes[player] = nil
end

-- Restores every tracked player. Collection first: Remove clears the key it was
-- reached by, and a pairs loop that loses a key skips the entry that slides
-- into the freed slot - which leaves hitboxes expanded with the toggle off.
function Hitbox.Clear()
    if not next(State.Hitboxes) then
        return
    end
    local held = {}
    for player in pairs(State.Hitboxes) do
        held[player] = true
    end
    for player in pairs(held) do
        Hitbox.Remove(player)
    end
end

function Hitbox.Apply(entry)
    if not entry or not entry.Character then
        return
    end

    local size = math.max(Config.HitboxSize, 1)
    local existing = State.Hitboxes[entry.Player]
    if existing then
        -- The slider can move while the expander is armed. Nothing re-runs
        -- Apply for an already-recorded player, so the size is what decides
        -- whether this is a no-op or a resize.
        --
        -- The record alone is not proof the work is still in place: a respawn
        -- hands the player a fresh rig at vanilla size while the record keeps
        -- pointing at the destroyed one, and trusting the record there leaves
        -- the new body untouched with the feature reading "on".
        local applied = existing.Size == size and #existing > 0
        if applied then
            for _, saved in ipairs(existing) do
                local part = saved.Part
                if not part or not part.Parent or math.abs(part.Size.X - size) > 0.05 then
                    applied = false
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if applied then
            return
        end
        Hitbox.Remove(entry.Player)
    end

    local saved = {}

    for _, name in ipairs(Hitbox.Names(Config.HitboxPart)) do
        local part = entry.Character:FindFirstChild(name)
        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
            table.insert(saved, {
                Part = part,
                Size = part.Size,
                Transparency = part.Transparency,
                Color = part.Color,
                CanCollide = part.CanCollide,
            })
            pcall(function()
                part.Size = Vector3.new(size, size, size)
                part.CanCollide = false
                if Config.HitboxEntityVisible then
                    part.Transparency = Config.HitboxTransparency
                    part.Color = Config.HitboxColor
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    if #saved > 0 then
        saved.Size = size
        State.Hitboxes[entry.Player] = saved
    end
end

function Hitbox.Update()
    if not Config.HitboxEnabled then
        Hitbox.Clear()
        return
    end

    for _, entry in ipairs(Cache.Get()) do
        if entry.Alive then
            if Config.AimTeamCheck and entry.Teammate then
                Hitbox.Remove(entry.Player)
            else
                Hitbox.Apply(entry)
            end
        else
            Hitbox.Remove(entry.Player)
        end
    end

    -- Dump hitboxes for players who left or died between cache rebuilds.
    -- Collected first: Remove clears the key, and clearing a key while a pairs
    -- loop walks the table silently skips the entry that fills the slot.
    local stale = {}
    for player in pairs(State.Hitboxes) do
        if not player.Parent or not PlayerUtil.IsAlive(player) then
            stale[player] = true
        end
    end
    for player in pairs(stale) do
        Hitbox.Remove(player)
    end
end

--==================================================
-- ESP
--==================================================

-- Every player owns one fixed set of drawing objects. Creating them on the
-- first sight of a player and destroying them when the player leaves keeps
-- the object count flat instead of churning `Drawing.new` every frame, which
-- is what eats the frame budget on a busy server.
local ESP = {}

ESP.Players = {}
ESP.Highlights = {}
ESP.Bones = {
    -- R15
    { "Head", "UpperTorso" },
    { "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso" },
    { "UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm" },
    { "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm" },
    { "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand" },
    { "UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm" },
    { "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm" },
    { "RightLowerArm", "RightHand" },
    { "LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg" },
    { "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg" },
    { "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot" },
    { "LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg" },
    { "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg" },
    { "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot" },
    -- R6 fallbacks resolve to the same names where the rig differs
    { "Head", "Torso" },
    { "Torso", "Left Arm" },
    { "Torso", "Right Arm" },
    { "Torso", "Left Leg" },
    { "Torso", "Right Leg" },
}

-- Box outlines are Lines with explicit endpoints rather than Squares/Quads.
-- Rectangle anchor conventions differ between executors (centre in some,
-- top-left in others), and a mismatch shifted every box away from the model
-- while names, tracers and skeletons stayed correct.
function ESP.HealthColor(fraction)
    fraction = Util.Clamp(fraction, 0, 1)
    if fraction > 0.5 then
        local t = (fraction - 0.5) * 2
        return Color3.fromRGB(
            math.floor(Util.Lerp(255, 90, t) + 0.5),
            math.floor(Util.Lerp(120, 220, t) + 0.5),
            90
        )
    end
    local t = fraction * 2
    return Color3.fromRGB(
        math.floor(Util.Lerp(220, 255, t) + 0.5),
        math.floor(Util.Lerp(60, 120, t) + 0.5),
        70
    )
end

-- Writes the four corners of an axis-aligned rectangle. Passing an explicit
-- colour keeps every caller from forgetting it when the quad is reused.
function ESP.SetQuad(quad, left, top, right, bottom, color)
    if not quad then
        return
    end

    quad.PointA = Vector2.new(left, top)
    quad.PointB = Vector2.new(right, top)
    quad.PointC = Vector2.new(right, bottom)
    quad.PointD = Vector2.new(left, bottom)

    if color then
        quad.Color = color
    end
end

function ESP.Create()
    if not Draw.Available then
        return nil
    end

    local objects = {
        Fill = Draw.New("Quad"),
        HealthBack = Draw.New("Line"),
        HealthBar = Draw.New("Line"),
        Name = Draw.New("Text"),
        Distance = Draw.New("Text"),
        Weapon = Draw.New("Text"),
        Info = Draw.New("Text"),
        HealthText = Draw.New("Text"),
        HeadDot = Draw.New("Circle"),
        Tracer = Draw.New("Line"),
        Arrow = Draw.New("Triangle"),
        Skeleton = {},
        Corners = {},
        BoxLines = {},
    }

    if objects.Fill then
        objects.Fill.Thickness = 0
        objects.Fill.Filled = true
        objects.Fill.Transparency = 0.85
    end
    if objects.HealthBack then
        objects.HealthBack.Thickness = 2
        objects.HealthBack.Color = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 24)
        objects.HealthBack.Transparency = 0.4
    end
    if objects.HealthBar then
        objects.HealthBar.Thickness = 2
    end
    if objects.Name then
        objects.Name.Center = true
        objects.Name.Outline = true
        objects.Name.Font = 2
    end
    if objects.Distance then
        objects.Distance.Center = true
        objects.Distance.Outline = true
        objects.Distance.Font = 2
    end
    if objects.Weapon then
        objects.Weapon.Center = true
        objects.Weapon.Outline = true
        objects.Weapon.Font = 2
    end
    if objects.HealthText then
        objects.HealthText.Center = true
        objects.HealthText.Outline = true
        objects.HealthText.Font = 2
    end
    if objects.Info then
        objects.Info.Center = true
        objects.Info.Outline = true
        objects.Info.Font = 2
    end
    if objects.HeadDot then
        objects.HeadDot.Thickness = 2
        objects.HeadDot.Filled = true
        objects.HeadDot.NumSides = 16
        objects.HeadDot.Radius = 4
    end
    if objects.Tracer then
        objects.Tracer.Thickness = 1
    end
    if objects.Arrow then
        objects.Arrow.Thickness = 1
    end

    for index = 1, #ESP.Bones do
        local line = Draw.New("Line")
        if line then
            line.Thickness = 1
            line.Transparency = 0.35
        end
        objects.Skeleton[index] = line
    end

    -- Eight segments, two per corner. A corner-only box stays readable on a
    -- crowded screen where four full edges turn into a wall of rectangles.
    for index = 1, 8 do
        local line = Draw.New("Line")
        if line then
            line.Thickness = 1
            line.Transparency = 0.05
        end
        objects.Corners[index] = line
    end

    -- Four full edges for the "Full" box style. Lines are used instead of a
    -- Square/Quad because line endpoints are unambiguous: the outline lands on
    -- the head/feet extents regardless of how an executor anchors rectangles.
    for index = 1, 4 do
        local line = Draw.New("Line")
        if line then
            line.Thickness = 1
        end
        objects.BoxLines[index] = line
    end

    return objects
end

-- Body parts used to build the box. The game's own HitboxHead/HitboxBody are
-- deliberately absent: the hitbox expander resizes them, and including them
-- would make every ESP box balloon to whatever the expander set.
local EXTENT_PARTS = {
    "Head",
    "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "Torso",
    "LeftUpperArm", "RightUpperArm",
    "LeftLowerArm", "RightLowerArm",
    "LeftUpperLeg", "RightUpperLeg",
    "LeftLowerLeg", "RightLowerLeg",
    "LeftFoot", "RightFoot",
    "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg",
}

-- Why this is not an eight-corner AABB anymore:
--
-- Projecting every corner of a world-space AABB is fine in a textbook, but a
-- character's AABB changes every frame as the limbs animate, so boxes visibly
-- breathe and jitter. Worse, any corner that drifts within a stud or two of
-- the camera divides by an almost-zero depth and lands tens of thousands of
-- pixels away; that produced the 122796x-236146 and negative-height boxes.
--
-- The stable axis on a humanoid is head-to-feet, so that is projected directly
-- (two points, both on the body centre line, both rejected when they are too
-- close to the near plane). Width is measured in world space perpendicular to
-- the camera and converted with the same perspective scale, which is immune to
-- near-plane blow-ups and does not change when an arm swings forward.
local ESP_NEAR = 2 -- studs; closer than this the projection is meaningless
local ESP_MAX_HEIGHT_WORLD = 9
local ESP_MAX_WIDTH_WORLD = 9

local function PixelsPerStud(depth)
    local camera = Cam.Get()
    if not camera or depth <= ESP_NEAR then
        return nil
    end

    local viewport = Cam.ViewportSize()
    local tanHalf = math.tan(math.rad(camera.FieldOfView) * 0.5)
    if tanHalf <= 1e-4 then
        return nil
    end

    return (viewport.Y * 0.5) / (depth * tanHalf)
end

local function CharacterExtents(character)
    local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")

    if not root or not head then
        return nil
    end

    local camera = Cam.Get()
    if not camera then
        return nil
    end

    -- Flattened camera right vector: the box width must stay meaningful even
    -- when the lobby camera looks almost straight down at the characters.
    local right = camera.CFrame.RightVector
    right = Vector3.new(right.X, 0, right.Z)
    if right.Magnitude < 1e-3 then
        right = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
    else
        right = right.Unit
    end

    local minY, maxY = math.huge, -math.huge
    local halfWidth = 0
    local found = 0

    for _, name in ipairs(EXTENT_PARTS) do
        local part = character:FindFirstChild(name)
        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
            local position = part.Position
            local half = part.Size * 0.5

            minY = math.min(minY, position.Y - half.Y)
            maxY = math.max(maxY, position.Y + half.Y)

            local offset = position - root.Position
            local extent = math.abs(right.X) * half.X + math.abs(right.Z) * half.Z
            halfWidth = math.max(halfWidth, math.abs(offset:Dot(right)) + extent)
            found = found + 1
        end
    end

    -- Rig names differ between R6/R15 and some games rename limbs entirely.
    -- A head/root estimate still gives a usable box instead of no box at all.
    if found < 3 then
        minY = root.Position.Y - root.Size.Y * 0.5 - 2.6
        maxY = head.Position.Y + head.Size.Y * 0.5
        halfWidth = math.max(root.Size.X, root.Size.Z) * 0.5 + 0.8
    end

    if minY == math.huge or maxY == -math.huge then
        minY = root.Position.Y - 3
        maxY = head.Position.Y + 0.6
        halfWidth = 1.5
    end

    local heightWorld = Util.Clamp(maxY - minY, 1, ESP_MAX_HEIGHT_WORLD)
    local widthWorld = Util.Clamp(halfWidth * 2, 1.5, ESP_MAX_WIDTH_WORLD)

    local axisX, axisZ = root.Position.X, root.Position.Z
    local bottomWorld = Vector3.new(axisX, minY, axisZ)
    local topWorld = Vector3.new(axisX, minY + heightWorld, axisZ)

    local topPoint, _, topDepth = Cam.WorldToScreen(topWorld)
    local bottomPoint, _, bottomDepth = Cam.WorldToScreen(bottomWorld)

    -- One failing point means at least one end of the body is on top of, or
    -- behind, the near plane. Returning nil hides the player for that frame
    -- rather than drawing a box made of garbage coordinates.
    if not topPoint or not bottomPoint or topDepth <= ESP_NEAR or bottomDepth <= ESP_NEAR then
        return nil
    end

    local scale = PixelsPerStud((topDepth + bottomDepth) * 0.5)
    if not scale then
        return nil
    end

    local top = topPoint.Y
    local bottom = bottomPoint.Y
    if top > bottom then
        top, bottom = bottom, top
    end

    local viewport = Cam.ViewportSize()

    -- Clamp the dimensions first, then derive the edges from the final width
    -- and height. Applying the height clamp after the edges were computed left
    -- the outline and the reported Size disagreeing, which showed up as a
    -- 1-2 px box whenever a distant or overhead character collapsed to a flat
    -- line.
    local width = Util.Clamp(widthWorld * scale, 4, viewport.X * 2)
    local height = bottom - top

    -- From almost directly above, head and feet collapse onto nearly the same
    -- pixel row. Give the box a minimum height so it never becomes a flat line.
    if height < width * 0.45 then
        height = width * 0.45
    end

    height = Util.Clamp(height, 5, viewport.Y * 2)

    local centerX = (topPoint.X + bottomPoint.X) * 0.5
    local centerY = (top + bottom) * 0.5

    local left = centerX - width * 0.5
    local rightEdge = centerX + width * 0.5
    local topEdge = centerY - height * 0.5
    local bottomEdge = centerY + height * 0.5

    -- Defensive: a NaN/Inf camera frame must never reach the Drawing API.
    if left ~= left or topEdge ~= topEdge or rightEdge ~= rightEdge or bottomEdge ~= bottomEdge then
        return nil
    end

    return {
        Left = left,
        Right = rightEdge,
        Top = topEdge,
        Bottom = bottomEdge,
        Center = Vector2.new(centerX, centerY),
        Size = Vector2.new(width, height),
    }
end

function ESP.Hide(objects)
    if not objects then
        return
    end
    for key, object in pairs(objects) do
        if key ~= "Skeleton" and key ~= "Corners" and key ~= "BoxLines" then
            Draw.Show(object, false)
        end
    end
    for _, line in ipairs(objects.Skeleton or {}) do
        Draw.Show(line, false)
    end
    for _, line in ipairs(objects.Corners or {}) do
        Draw.Show(line, false)
    end
    for _, line in ipairs(objects.BoxLines or {}) do
        Draw.Show(line, false)
    end

    -- Invisible drawings keep their last geometry. Collapse the fill quad so a
    -- parked entry cannot report a stale (and possibly huge) rectangle.
    ESP.SetQuad(objects.Fill, 0, 0, 0, 0)
end

function ESP.Destroy(player)
    local objects = ESP.Players[player]
    if objects then
        for key, object in pairs(objects) do
            if key == "Skeleton" or key == "Corners" or key == "BoxLines" then
                for _, line in ipairs(object) do
                    Draw.Remove(line)
                end
            else
                Draw.Remove(object)
            end
        end
        ESP.Players[player] = nil
    end

    local highlight = ESP.Highlights[player]
    if highlight then
        pcall(function()
            highlight:Destroy()
        end)
        ESP.Highlights[player] = nil
    end
end

function ESP.ClearAll()
    local players = {}
    for player in pairs(ESP.Players) do
        players[player] = true
    end
    for player in pairs(ESP.Highlights) do
        players[player] = true
    end
    for player in pairs(players) do
        ESP.Destroy(player)
    end
end

function ESP.UpdateHighlight(entry, player)
    local blocked = not Config.ESPHighlights
        or not Config.ESPEnabled
        or not entry
        or not entry.Character
        or not entry.Alive
        or entry.Distance > Config.ESPMaxDistance
        or (Config.ESPTeamCheck and entry.Teammate)

    if blocked then
        local existing = ESP.Highlights[player]
        if existing then
            pcall(function()
                existing:Destroy()
            end)
            ESP.Highlights[player] = nil
        end
        return
    end

    local highlight = ESP.Highlights[player]

    if not highlight or highlight.Parent == nil or highlight.Adornee ~= entry.Character then
        if highlight then
            pcall(function()
                highlight:Destroy()
            end)
        end

        highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
        highlight.Name = "RIVALS_ESP_" .. player.Name
        highlight.DepthMode = Config.ESPVisibleOnly
            and Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
            or Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        highlight.Adornee = entry.Character
        highlight.Parent = ScreenGui

        ESP.Highlights[player] = highlight
    end

    highlight.FillColor = Config.ESPHighlightColor
    highlight.FillTransparency = 0.55
    highlight.OutlineColor = Config.ESPHighlightColor
    highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0.1
end

-- Skeleton pass. Both rigs are handled by asking for each pair and drawing
-- only the ones whose two parts actually exist, so an R6 avatar simply skips
-- the R15 rows.
function ESP.DrawSkeleton(objects, character, color)
    for index, pair in ipairs(ESP.Bones) do
        local line = objects.Skeleton[index]
        if line then
            local first = character:FindFirstChild(pair[1])
            local second = character:FindFirstChild(pair[2])

            if first and second and first:IsA("BasePart") and second:IsA("BasePart") then
                local a, _, az = Cam.WorldToScreen(first.Position)
                local b, _, bz = Cam.WorldToScreen(second.Position)

                if a and b and az > 0 and bz > 0 then
                    line.From = a
                    line.To = b
                    line.Color = color
                    line.Visible = true
                else
                    line.Visible = false
                end
            else
                line.Visible = false
            end
        end
    end
end

-- Four full edges drawn as Lines. This is the default box style; using lines
-- keeps the outline on the head/feet extents even when a Square/Quad's anchor
-- convention differs between executors.
function ESP.DrawEdges(objects, extents, color)
    local lines = objects.BoxLines
    if not lines then
        return
    end

    local left, right = extents.Left, extents.Right
    local top, bottom = extents.Top, extents.Bottom
    local line

    line = lines[1]
    if line then
        line.From = Vector2.new(left, top)
        line.To = Vector2.new(right, top)
        line.Color = color
        line.Visible = true
    end

    line = lines[2]
    if line then
        line.From = Vector2.new(right, top)
        line.To = Vector2.new(right, bottom)
        line.Color = color
        line.Visible = true
    end

    line = lines[3]
    if line then
        line.From = Vector2.new(right, bottom)
        line.To = Vector2.new(left, bottom)
        line.Color = color
        line.Visible = true
    end

    line = lines[4]
    if line then
        line.From = Vector2.new(left, bottom)
        line.To = Vector2.new(left, top)
        line.Color = color
        line.Visible = true
    end
end

-- Two segments per corner: a horizontal and a vertical stub. Drawn instead of
-- the full outline when the user picks the Corners style.
function ESP.DrawCorners(objects, extents, color)
    local lines = objects.Corners
    if not lines then
        return
    end

    local left, right = extents.Left, extents.Right
    local top, bottom = extents.Top, extents.Bottom
    local lengthX = math.min((right - left) * 0.3, 22)
    local lengthY = math.min((bottom - top) * 0.3, 22)
    local line

    line = lines[1]
    if line then
        line.From = Vector2.new(left, top + lengthY)
        line.To = Vector2.new(left, top)
        line.Color = color
        line.Visible = true
    end

    line = lines[2]
    if line then
        line.From = Vector2.new(left, top)
        line.To = Vector2.new(left + lengthX, top)
        line.Color = color
        line.Visible = true
    end

    line = lines[3]
    if line then
        line.From = Vector2.new(right - lengthX, top)
        line.To = Vector2.new(right, top)
        line.Color = color
        line.Visible = true
    end

    line = lines[4]
    if line then
        line.From = Vector2.new(right, top)
        line.To = Vector2.new(right, top + lengthY)
        line.Color = color
        line.Visible = true
    end

    line = lines[5]
    if line then
        line.From = Vector2.new(left, bottom - lengthY)
        line.To = Vector2.new(left, bottom)
        line.Color = color
        line.Visible = true
    end

    line = lines[6]
    if line then
        line.From = Vector2.new(left, bottom)
        line.To = Vector2.new(left + lengthX, bottom)
        line.Color = color
        line.Visible = true
    end

    line = lines[7]
    if line then
        line.From = Vector2.new(right - lengthX, bottom)
        line.To = Vector2.new(right, bottom)
        line.Color = color
        line.Visible = true
    end

    line = lines[8]
    if line then
        line.From = Vector2.new(right, bottom)
        line.To = Vector2.new(right, bottom - lengthY)
        line.Color = color
        line.Visible = true
    end
end

function ESP.DrawArrow(objects, screen, color)
    local arrow = objects.Arrow
    if not arrow then
        return
    end

    local viewport = Cam.ViewportSize()
    local center = Cam.ScreenCenter()

    local clampedX = Util.Clamp(screen.X, 30, viewport.X - 30)
    local clampedY = Util.Clamp(screen.Y, 30, viewport.Y - 30)

    local direction = (screen - center)
    if direction.Magnitude < 1 then
        direction = Vector2.new(0, -1)
    end
    direction = direction.Unit

    local anchor = Vector2.new(clampedX, clampedY) - direction * 20
    local perpendicular = Vector2.new(-direction.Y, direction.X)

    arrow.PointA = anchor + direction * 14
    arrow.PointB = anchor - direction * 6 + perpendicular * 9
    arrow.PointC = anchor - direction * 6 - perpendicular * 9
    arrow.Color = color
    arrow.Filled = true
    arrow.Visible = true
end

function ESP.Render(entry)
    local player = entry.Player
    local character = entry.Character

    -- Highlight first. Every early return below used to skip it, which is how
    -- a dead or out-of-range player kept a stale full-body outline forever.
    ESP.UpdateHighlight(entry, player)

    if not Config.ESPEnabled then
        ESP.Hide(ESP.Players[player])
        return
    end

    if not entry.Alive and not Config.ESPSkeleton then
        ESP.Hide(ESP.Players[player])
        return
    end

    if entry.Distance > Config.ESPMaxDistance then
        ESP.Hide(ESP.Players[player])
        return
    end

    if Config.ESPTeamCheck and entry.Teammate then
        ESP.Hide(ESP.Players[player])
        return
    end

    if Config.ESPVisibleOnly and not PlayerUtil.IsVisible(player) then
        ESP.Hide(ESP.Players[player])
        return
    end

    local extents = CharacterExtents(character)
    if not extents then
        ESP.Hide(ESP.Players[player])
        return
    end

    local objects = ESP.Players[player]
    if not objects then
        objects = ESP.Create()
        ESP.Players[player] = objects
    end

    if not objects then
        return
    end

    local healthFraction = entry.MaxHealth > 0 and (entry.Health / entry.MaxHealth) or 0
    local boxColor

    -- Priority order: the aimlock target, then teammates (when the filter is
    -- off), then the health ramp, then the flat user colour. One target can
    -- only have one colour, so the first match wins.
    if Config.ESPTargetColor and State.Target and State.Target.Player == player then
        boxColor = Config.ESPTargetColorValue
    elseif Config.ESPTeamColors and entry.Teammate then
        boxColor = Config.ESPTeamColorValue
    elseif Config.ESPHealth then
        boxColor = ESP.HealthColor(healthFraction)
    else
        boxColor = Config.ESPBoxColor
    end

    if Config.ESPBoxes then
        if Config.ESPBoxStyle == "Corners" then
            for _, line in ipairs(objects.BoxLines) do
                Draw.Show(line, false)
            end
            ESP.DrawCorners(objects, extents, boxColor)
        else
            ESP.DrawEdges(objects, extents, boxColor)

            for _, line in ipairs(objects.Corners) do
                Draw.Show(line, false)
            end
        end
    else
        for _, line in ipairs(objects.BoxLines) do
            Draw.Show(line, false)
        end
        for _, line in ipairs(objects.Corners) do
            Draw.Show(line, false)
        end
    end

    if Config.ESPBoxFill and Config.ESPBoxes and objects.Fill then
        ESP.SetQuad(
            objects.Fill,
            extents.Left,
            extents.Top,
            extents.Right,
            extents.Bottom,
            Config.ESPBoxFillColor
        )
        objects.Fill.Transparency = Config.ESPBoxFillTransparency
        objects.Fill.Visible = true
    elseif objects.Fill then
        objects.Fill.Visible = false
    end

    if Config.ESPHealthBar then
        local barX = extents.Left - 5

        objects.HealthBack.From = Vector2.new(barX, extents.Top)
        objects.HealthBack.To = Vector2.new(barX, extents.Bottom)
        objects.HealthBack.Visible = true

        local filled = extents.Size.Y * Util.Clamp(healthFraction, 0, 1)
        objects.HealthBar.From = Vector2.new(barX, extents.Bottom)
        objects.HealthBar.To = Vector2.new(barX, extents.Bottom - filled)
        objects.HealthBar.Color = ESP.HealthColor(healthFraction)
        objects.HealthBar.Visible = true
    else
        objects.HealthBack.Visible = false
        objects.HealthBar.Visible = false
    end

    if Config.ESPNames then
        local text = Config.ESPDisplayName and entry.DisplayName or entry.Name
        objects.Name.Text = text
        objects.Name.Size = Config.ESPTextSize
        objects.Name.Position = Vector2.new(extents.Center.X, extents.Top - 14)
        objects.Name.Color = Config.ESPNameColor
        objects.Name.Visible = true
    else
        objects.Name.Visible = false
    end

    if Config.ESPDistance then
        objects.Distance.Text = string.format("%d m", math.floor(entry.Distance / 3 + 0.5))
        objects.Distance.Size = Config.ESPTextSize - 2
        objects.Distance.Position = Vector2.new(extents.Center.X, extents.Bottom + 10)
        objects.Distance.Color = Config.ESPNameColor
        objects.Distance.Visible = true
    else
        objects.Distance.Visible = false
    end

    if Config.ESPHealth and Config.ESPHealthBar == false then
        objects.HealthText.Text = string.format("%d", math.floor(entry.Health + 0.5))
        objects.HealthText.Size = Config.ESPTextSize - 2
        objects.HealthText.Position = Vector2.new(extents.Center.X, extents.Bottom + 22)
        objects.HealthText.Color = ESP.HealthColor(healthFraction)
        objects.HealthText.Visible = true
    else
        objects.HealthText.Visible = false
    end

    if Config.ESPWeapon and entry.Weapon then
        objects.Weapon.Text = Util.Readable(entry.Weapon)
        objects.Weapon.Size = Config.ESPTextSize - 2
        objects.Weapon.Position = Vector2.new(extents.Center.X, extents.Top - 26)
        objects.Weapon.Color = Config.ESPNameColor
        objects.Weapon.Visible = true
    else
        objects.Weapon.Visible = false
    end

    if Config.ESPInfo then
        local bits = {}
        if type(entry.Level) == "number" then
            table.insert(bits, string.format("Lv %d", math.floor(entry.Level)))
        end
        if type(entry.ELO) == "number" then
            table.insert(bits, string.format("ELO %d", math.floor(entry.ELO)))
        end
        if type(entry.Status) == "string" and entry.Status ~= "" then
            table.insert(bits, entry.Status)
        end
        if #bits > 0 then
            objects.Info.Text = table.concat(bits, "  ")
            objects.Info.Size = Config.ESPTextSize - 2
            objects.Info.Position = Vector2.new(extents.Center.X, extents.Bottom + 34)
            objects.Info.Color = Config.ESPNameColor
            objects.Info.Visible = true
        else
            objects.Info.Visible = false
        end
    else
        objects.Info.Visible = false
    end

    if Config.ESPHeadDot then
        local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head then
            local screen, _, z = Cam.WorldToScreen(head.Position)
            if screen and z > 0 then
                objects.HeadDot.Position = screen
                objects.HeadDot.Color = Config.ESPHeadDotColor
                objects.HeadDot.Visible = true
            else
                objects.HeadDot.Visible = false
            end
        else
            objects.HeadDot.Visible = false
        end
    else
        objects.HeadDot.Visible = false
    end

    if Config.ESPTracers then
        local viewport = Cam.ViewportSize()
        local origin

        if Config.ESPTracerOrigin == "Mouse" then
            origin = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
        elseif Config.ESPTracerOrigin == "Top" then
            origin = Vector2.new(viewport.X / 2, 0)
        else
            origin = Vector2.new(viewport.X / 2, viewport.Y)
        end

        objects.Tracer.From = origin
        objects.Tracer.To = Vector2.new(extents.Center.X, extents.Bottom)
        objects.Tracer.Color = Config.ESPTracerColor
        objects.Tracer.Thickness = 1
        objects.Tracer.Visible = true
    else
        objects.Tracer.Visible = false
    end

    if Config.ESPSkeleton then
        ESP.DrawSkeleton(objects, character, Config.ESPSkeletonColor)
    else
        for _, line in ipairs(objects.Skeleton) do
            Draw.Show(line, false)
        end
    end

    if Config.ESPArrows then
        local root = entry.Root
        local screen, onScreen, z = Cam.WorldToScreen(root.Position)
        if screen and (not onScreen or z <= 0) then
            ESP.DrawArrow(objects, screen, Config.ESPArrowColor)
        elseif objects.Arrow then
            objects.Arrow.Visible = false
        end
    elseif objects.Arrow then
        objects.Arrow.Visible = false
    end

    ESP.UpdateHighlight(entry, player)
end

function ESP.Update()
    if not Draw.Available then
        return
    end

    local entries = Cache.Get()
    local seen = {}

    for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
        seen[entry.Player] = true
        ESP.Render(entry)
    end

    -- Collected before removal. Destroy clears the key it was reached by, so
    -- walking either table directly skips whatever slides into the freed slot
    -- and leaves its drawings on screen after the player is gone.
    local stale = {}
    for player in pairs(ESP.Players) do
        if not seen[player] then
            stale[player] = true
        end
    end
    for player in pairs(ESP.Highlights) do
        if not seen[player] then
            stale[player] = true
        end
    end
    for player in pairs(stale) do
        ESP.Destroy(player)
    end
end

-- Live-probe handle: the merged script keeps everything in locals, so this is
-- the only way to inspect the drawing table from the executor without
-- enumerating the registry.
_G.RIVALS_ESP = ESP

--==================================================
-- RADAR
--==================================================

local Radar = {}

Radar.Border = nil
Radar.Back = nil
Radar.Dots = {}
Radar.Labels = {}

function Radar.Init()
    if not Draw.Available then
        return
    end

    Radar.Border = Draw.New("Quad")
    Radar.Back = Draw.New("Quad")

    if Radar.Border then
        Radar.Border.Filled = false
        Radar.Border.Thickness = 1
    end

    if Radar.Back then
        Radar.Back.Filled = true
        Radar.Back.Thickness = 0
        Radar.Back.Color = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 14)
    end
end

function Radar.Anchor(size)
    local viewport = Cam.ViewportSize()
    local margin = 24

    if Config.RadarPosition == "TopLeft" then
        return Vector2.new(margin + size / 2, margin + size / 2)
    elseif Config.RadarPosition == "BottomLeft" then
        return Vector2.new(margin + size / 2, viewport.Y - margin - size / 2)
    elseif Config.RadarPosition == "BottomRight" then
        return Vector2.new(viewport.X - margin - size / 2, viewport.Y - margin - size / 2)
    end

    return Vector2.new(viewport.X - margin - size / 2, margin + size / 2)
end

function Radar.Update()
    if not Draw.Available or not Radar.Border then
        return
    end

    local size = Config.RadarSize
    local center = Radar.Anchor(size)
    local half = size * 0.5
    local left = center.X - half
    local right = center.X + half
    local top = center.Y - half
    local bottom = center.Y + half

    Radar.Border.PointA = Vector2.new(left, top)
    Radar.Border.PointB = Vector2.new(right, top)
    Radar.Border.PointC = Vector2.new(right, bottom)
    Radar.Border.PointD = Vector2.new(left, bottom)
    Radar.Border.Color = Config.RadarColor
    Radar.Border.Visible = Config.ESPRadar

    Radar.Back.PointA = Vector2.new(left, top)
    Radar.Back.PointB = Vector2.new(right, top)
    Radar.Back.PointC = Vector2.new(right, bottom)
    Radar.Back.PointD = Vector2.new(left, bottom)
    Radar.Back.Color = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 14)
    Radar.Back.Transparency = 1 - Config.RadarOpacity
    Radar.Back.Visible = Config.ESPRadar

    if not Config.ESPRadar then
        for _, dot in pairs(Radar.Dots) do
            dot.Visible = false
        end
        for _, label in pairs(Radar.Labels) do
            label.Visible = false
        end
        return
    end

    local localRoot = PlayerUtil.GetRoot(LocalPlayer)
    local origin = localRoot and localRoot.Position or Cam.Position()
    local yaw = math.rad(Cam.Angles().X)

    local seen = {}

    for _, entry in ipairs(Cache.Get()) do
        seen[entry.Player] = true

        local dot = Radar.Dots[entry.Player]
        if not dot then
            dot = Draw.New("Circle")
            if dot then
                dot.Filled = true
                dot.NumSides = 10
                dot.Radius = 3
            end
            Radar.Dots[entry.Player] = dot
        end

        local label = Radar.Labels[entry.Player]
        if not label then
            label = Draw.New("Text")
            if label then
                label.Center = true
                label.Outline = true
                label.Font = 2
                label.Size = 11
            end
            Radar.Labels[entry.Player] = label
        end

        if dot and label then
            local valid = entry.Alive
                and entry.Distance <= Config.RadarRange
                and not (Config.ESPTeamCheck and entry.Teammate)

            if valid then
                local delta = entry.Root.Position - origin

                if Config.RadarRotate then
                    local cos, sin = math.cos(-yaw), math.sin(-yaw)
                    local rotatedX = delta.X * cos - delta.Z * sin
                    local rotatedZ = delta.X * sin + delta.Z * cos
                    delta = Vector3.new(rotatedX, 0, rotatedZ)
                end

                -- radar is top-down: forward is up, so Z maps to negative Y
                local scale = (size / 2) / math.max(Config.RadarRange, 1)
                local point = Vector2.new(
                    center.X + delta.X * scale,
                    center.Y + delta.Z * scale
                )

                local magnitude = (point - center).Magnitude
                if magnitude > size / 2 - 4 then
                    local direction = (point - center)
                    if direction.Magnitude > 0.001 then
                        point = center + direction.Unit * (size / 2 - 4)
                    end
                end

                dot.Position = point
                dot.Radius = 3
                dot.Color = entry.Teammate
                    and Color3.fromRGB(90, 200, 120)
                    or Config.RadarColor
                dot.Transparency = 0.15
                dot.Visible = true

                label.Text = entry.Name
                label.Position = point + Vector2.new(0, 9)
                label.Color = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 240)
                label.Transparency = 0.2
                label.Visible = Config.RadarSize >= 150
            else
                dot.Visible = false
                label.Visible = false
            end
        end
    end

    -- Collected before removal, same reason as the ESP sweep: the keys go away
    -- as they are handled, and a pairs loop cannot survive that.
    local staleDots = {}
    for player in pairs(Radar.Dots) do
        if not seen[player] then
            staleDots[player] = true
        end
    end
    for player in pairs(Radar.Labels) do
        if not seen[player] then
            staleDots[player] = true
        end
    end
    for player in pairs(staleDots) do
        Draw.Remove(Radar.Dots[player])
        Radar.Dots[player] = nil
        Draw.Remove(Radar.Labels[player])
        Radar.Labels[player] = nil
    end
end

function Radar.Clear()
    local dots = {}
    for player in pairs(Radar.Dots) do
        dots[player] = true
    end
    for player in pairs(Radar.Labels) do
        dots[player] = true
    end
    for player in pairs(dots) do
        Draw.Remove(Radar.Dots[player])
        Radar.Dots[player] = nil
        Draw.Remove(Radar.Labels[player])
        Radar.Labels[player] = nil
    end
    Draw.Remove(Radar.Border)
    Draw.Remove(Radar.Back)
    Radar.Border = nil
    Radar.Back = nil
end

--==================================================
-- CHAMS
--==================================================

-- Two paths, because they do different jobs:
--   Highlight  - an adornee that renders through geometry without touching
--                the rig. DepthMode carries the through-walls decision.
--   Solid      - the parts themselves are recoloured to a flat material and
--                restored from a snapshot when the toggle drops. This is the
--                one that actually changes the look of the model.
local Chams = {}

Chams.Highlights = {}
Chams.Snapshots = {}

local ChamRestoreProperties = {
    "Color",
    "Material",
    "Transparency",
    "Reflectance",
}

local function SnapshotPart(part)
    local snapshot = {}
    for _, property in ipairs(ChamRestoreProperties) do
        local ok, value = pcall(function()
            return part[property]
        end)
        if ok then
            snapshot[property] = value
        end
    end
    return snapshot
end

local function RestorePart(part, snapshot)
    if not part or not part.Parent or not snapshot then
        return
    end
    for property, value in pairs(snapshot) do
        pcall(function()
            part[property] = value
        end)
    end
end

function Chams.ClearPlayer(player)
    local highlight = Chams.Highlights[player]
    if highlight then
        pcall(function()
            highlight:Destroy()
        end)
        Chams.Highlights[player] = nil
    end

    local snapshot = Chams.Snapshots[player]
    if snapshot then
        for part, data in pairs(snapshot) do
            RestorePart(part, data)
        end
        Chams.Snapshots[player] = nil
    end
end

function Chams.Clear()
    -- Collect first: ClearPlayer removes keys, and removing keys while a pairs
    -- loop is walking the same table skips entries.
    local players = {}
    for player in pairs(Chams.Highlights) do
        players[player] = true
    end
    for player in pairs(Chams.Snapshots) do
        players[player] = true
    end
    for player in pairs(players) do
        Chams.ClearPlayer(player)
    end
end

-- Drops the highlight without touching the part snapshot, for the paths that
-- only need to retire the silhouette (solid mode with walls off).
function Chams.ClearHighlight(player)
    local highlight = Chams.Highlights[player]
    if highlight then
        pcall(function()
            highlight:Destroy()
        end)
        Chams.Highlights[player] = nil
    end
end

-- Drops the recolour without touching the highlight, for a mode switch from
-- Solid back to Highlight.
function Chams.RestoreSolid(player)
    local snapshot = Chams.Snapshots[player]
    if not snapshot then
        return
    end
    for part, data in pairs(snapshot) do
        if type(part) == "Instance" then
            RestorePart(part, data)
        end
    end
    Chams.Snapshots[player] = nil
end

function Chams.ApplyHighlight(entry)
    local player = entry.Player
    local character = entry.Character

    local highlight = Chams.Highlights[player]

    if not highlight or highlight.Parent == nil or highlight.Adornee ~= character then
        if highlight then
            pcall(function()
                highlight:Destroy()
            end)
        end

        highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
        highlight.Name = "RIVALS_Cham_" .. player.Name
        highlight.Adornee = character
        highlight.Parent = ScreenGui
        Chams.Highlights[player] = highlight
    end

    local mode = Config.ChamsMode

    if mode == "Wireframe" then
        highlight.FillTransparency = 1
        highlight.OutlineColor = Config.ChamsOutlineColor
        highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0.05
    else
        highlight.FillColor = Config.ChamsFillColor
        highlight.FillTransparency = Config.ChamsFillTransparency
        highlight.OutlineColor = Config.ChamsOutlineColor
        highlight.OutlineTransparency = Config.ChamsOutlineTransparency
    end

    highlight.DepthMode = Config.ChamsWalls
        and Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        or Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
end

function Chams.ApplySolid(entry)
    local player = entry.Player
    local character = entry.Character

    local snapshot = Chams.Snapshots[player]

    if not snapshot or snapshot._character ~= character then
        if snapshot then
            for part, data in pairs(snapshot) do
                if type(part) == "Instance" then
                    RestorePart(part, data)
                end
            end
        end

        snapshot = { _character = character }
        Chams.Snapshots[player] = snapshot
    end

    local material = Enum.Material.ForceField
    local okMaterial = pcall(function()
        material = Enum.Material[Config.ChamsSolidMaterial]
    end)
    if not okMaterial then
        material = Enum.Material.ForceField
    end

    for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") and not part:IsA("Model") then
            if not snapshot[part] then
                snapshot[part] = SnapshotPart(part)
            end
            pcall(function()
                part.Material = material
                part.Color = Config.ChamsFillColor
                part.Transparency = Config.ChamsSolidTransparency
            end)
        end
    end

    -- The highlight still runs in solid mode so the silhouette reads through
    -- walls; the parts only carry the flat colour.
    if Config.ChamsWalls then
        local highlight = Chams.Highlights[player]
        if not highlight or highlight.Adornee ~= character then
            if highlight then
                pcall(function()
                    highlight:Destroy()
                end)
            end
            highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
            highlight.Name = "RIVALS_ChamSolid_" .. player.Name
            highlight.Adornee = character
            highlight.Parent = ScreenGui
            Chams.Highlights[player] = highlight
        end
        highlight.FillTransparency = 1
        highlight.OutlineColor = Config.ChamsFillColor
        highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0.25
        highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    else
        -- Walls off means no silhouette at all. Leaving the one created while
        -- walls were on would keep the outline visible with nothing toggled on.
        Chams.ClearHighlight(player)
    end
end

function Chams.Update()
    if not Config.ChamsEnabled then
        if next(Chams.Highlights) or next(Chams.Snapshots) then
            Chams.Clear()
        end
        return
    end

    local seen = {}

    for _, entry in ipairs(Cache.Get()) do
        seen[entry.Player] = true

        local valid = entry.Alive
            and entry.Distance <= Config.ChamsMaxDistance
            and not (Config.ChamsTeamCheck and entry.Teammate)

        if valid then
            if Config.ChamsMode == "Solid" then
                -- A mode switch leaves the other path's state behind: solid
                -- recolours every part and the highlight path never restores
                -- them, so the model would stay ForceField-coloured with the
                -- dropdown reading Highlight.
                Chams.ClearHighlight(player)
                Chams.ApplySolid(entry)
            else
                Chams.RestoreSolid(player)
                Chams.ApplyHighlight(entry)
            end
        else
            Chams.ClearPlayer(entry.Player)
        end
    end

    -- Collect before removing. ClearPlayer deletes both keys, so walking either
    -- table directly would skip the entry that shifts into the freed slot.
    local stale = {}
    for player in pairs(Chams.Highlights) do
        if not seen[player] then
            stale[player] = true
        end
    end
    for player in pairs(Chams.Snapshots) do
        if not seen[player] then
            stale[player] = true
        end
    end
    for player in pairs(stale) do
        Chams.ClearPlayer(player)
    end
end

--==================================================
-- CROSSHAIR
--==================================================

local Crosshair = {}

Crosshair.Lines = {}
Crosshair.Dot = nil
Crosshair.Circle = nil
Crosshair.Angle = 0

function Crosshair.Init()
    if not Draw.Available then
        return
    end

    for index = 1, 8 do
        local line = Draw.New("Line")
        if line then
            line.Thickness = 1
        end
        Crosshair.Lines[index] = line
    end

    Crosshair.Dot = Draw.New("Circle")
    if Crosshair.Dot then
        Crosshair.Dot.Filled = true
        Crosshair.Dot.NumSides = 12
    end

    Crosshair.Circle = Draw.New("Circle")
    if Crosshair.Circle then
        Crosshair.Circle.Filled = false
        Crosshair.Circle.NumSides = 48
        Crosshair.Circle.Thickness = 1
    end
end

function Crosshair.HideAll()
    for _, line in ipairs(Crosshair.Lines) do
        Draw.Show(line, false)
    end
    Draw.Show(Crosshair.Dot, false)
    Draw.Show(Crosshair.Circle, false)
end

function Crosshair.SetLine(index, from, to, color, transparency, thickness)
    local line = Crosshair.Lines[index]
    if not line then
        return
    end
    line.From = from
    line.To = to
    line.Color = color
    line.Transparency = transparency
    line.Thickness = thickness
    line.Visible = true
end

function Crosshair.Update(delta)
    if not Draw.Available then
        return
    end

    if not Config.CrosshairEnabled then
        Crosshair.HideAll()
        return
    end

    local center = Cam.ScreenCenter()
    local size = Config.CrosshairSize
    local gap = Config.CrosshairGap
    local color = Config.CrosshairColor
    local transparency = 1 - Config.CrosshairTransparency
    local thickness = Config.CrosshairThickness
    local style = Config.CrosshairStyle

    if Config.CrosshairSpin then
        Crosshair.Angle = Crosshair.Angle
            + math.rad(Config.CrosshairSpinSpeed) * (delta or 0)
    end

    for _, line in ipairs(Crosshair.Lines) do
        line.Visible = false
    end

    if style == "Cross" then
        Crosshair.SetLine(1,
            Vector2.new(center.X - gap - size, center.Y),
            Vector2.new(center.X - gap, center.Y), color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(2,
            Vector2.new(center.X + gap, center.Y),
            Vector2.new(center.X + gap + size, center.Y), color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(3,
            Vector2.new(center.X, center.Y - gap - size),
            Vector2.new(center.X, center.Y - gap), color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(4,
            Vector2.new(center.X, center.Y + gap),
            Vector2.new(center.X, center.Y + gap + size), color, transparency, thickness)
    elseif style == "Dot" then
        if Crosshair.Dot then
            Crosshair.Dot.Position = center
            Crosshair.Dot.Radius = math.max(size * 0.35, 1)
            Crosshair.Dot.Color = color
            Crosshair.Dot.Transparency = transparency
            Crosshair.Dot.Visible = true
        end
    elseif style == "Circle" then
        if Crosshair.Circle then
            Crosshair.Circle.Position = center
            Crosshair.Circle.Radius = size
            Crosshair.Circle.Color = color
            Crosshair.Circle.Transparency = transparency
            Crosshair.Circle.Thickness = thickness
            Crosshair.Circle.Visible = true
        end
    elseif style == "T" then
        Crosshair.SetLine(1,
            Vector2.new(center.X - gap - size, center.Y),
            Vector2.new(center.X + gap + size, center.Y), color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(2,
            Vector2.new(center.X, center.Y + gap),
            Vector2.new(center.X, center.Y + gap + size), color, transparency, thickness)
    elseif style == "X" then
        local offset = Vector2.new(math.cos(math.rad(45)), math.sin(math.rad(45))) * size
        Crosshair.SetLine(1, center - offset, center - offset.Unit * gap,
            color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(2, center + offset, center + offset.Unit * gap,
            color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(3,
            center + Vector2.new(offset.X, -offset.Y),
            center + Vector2.new(offset.X, -offset.Y).Unit * gap,
            color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(4,
            center + Vector2.new(-offset.X, offset.Y),
            center + Vector2.new(-offset.X, offset.Y).Unit * gap,
            color, transparency, thickness)
    elseif style == "Corners" then
        local half = size
        local arm = size * 0.5
        Crosshair.SetLine(1,
            center + Vector2.new(-half, -half),
            center + Vector2.new(-half + arm, -half), color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(2,
            center + Vector2.new(-half, -half),
            center + Vector2.new(-half, -half + arm), color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(3,
            center + Vector2.new(half, -half),
            center + Vector2.new(half - arm, -half), color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(4,
            center + Vector2.new(half, -half),
            center + Vector2.new(half, -half + arm), color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(5,
            center + Vector2.new(-half, half),
            center + Vector2.new(-half + arm, half), color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(6,
            center + Vector2.new(-half, half),
            center + Vector2.new(-half, half - arm), color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(7,
            center + Vector2.new(half, half),
            center + Vector2.new(half - arm, half), color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(8,
            center + Vector2.new(half, half),
            center + Vector2.new(half, half - arm), color, transparency, thickness)
    elseif style == "Spinning" then
        local cos = math.cos(Crosshair.Angle)
        local sin = math.sin(Crosshair.Angle)
        local outward = Vector2.new(cos, sin)
        local perpendicular = Vector2.new(-sin, cos)

        Crosshair.SetLine(1,
            center + outward * gap,
            center + outward * (gap + size), color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(2,
            center - outward * gap,
            center - outward * (gap + size), color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(3,
            center + perpendicular * gap,
            center + perpendicular * (gap + size), color, transparency, thickness)
        Crosshair.SetLine(4,
            center - perpendicular * gap,
            center - perpendicular * (gap + size), color, transparency, thickness)
    end
end

--==================================================
-- VIEWMODEL / CAMERA FOV
--==================================================

local Viewmodel = {}

Viewmodel.LastApply = 0
Viewmodel.WasEnabled = false

-- The game writes its own FieldOfView every frame in some camera scripts, so
-- the value is re-applied on a low-frequency timer rather than once. The
-- restore only fires on the frame the toggle drops, so it never fights a
-- scope or a camera animation the game is running.
function Viewmodel.Update()
    local camera = Cam.Get()
    if not camera then
        return
    end

    if Config.CameraFOVEnabled then
        if os.clock() - Viewmodel.LastApply < 0.1 then
            return
        end
        Viewmodel.LastApply = os.clock()
        if not Viewmodel.WasEnabled then
            -- Snapshot the live value at the moment of enable: the backup taken
            -- at load is the menu-time FOV, not whatever the game is running.
            Backup.CameraFOV = camera.FieldOfView
        end
        Viewmodel.WasEnabled = true

        pcall(function()
            camera.FieldOfView = Config.CameraFOV
        end)
    elseif Viewmodel.WasEnabled then
        Viewmodel.WasEnabled = false

        pcall(function()
            camera.FieldOfView = Backup.CameraFOV or 70
        end)
    end
end

--==================================================
-- HUD
--==================================================

local Hud = {}

Hud.Watermark = nil
Hud.Stats = nil
Hud.Keybinds = nil
Hud.Lines = {}

function Hud.Init()
    if not Draw.Available then
        return
    end

    Hud.Watermark = Draw.New("Text")
    if Hud.Watermark then
        Hud.Watermark.Font = 3
        Hud.Watermark.Size = 16
        Hud.Watermark.Outline = true
    end

    Hud.Stats = Draw.New("Text")
    if Hud.Stats then
        Hud.Stats.Font = 2
        Hud.Stats.Size = 14
        Hud.Stats.Outline = true
    end

    Hud.Keybinds = Draw.New("Text")
    if Hud.Keybinds then
        Hud.Keybinds.Font = 2
        Hud.Keybinds.Size = 13
        Hud.Keybinds.Outline = true
    end

    for index = 1, 24 do
        local line = Draw.New("Text")
        if line then
            line.Font = 2
            line.Size = 12
            line.Outline = true
        end
        Hud.Lines[index] = line
    end
end

function Hud.StatsAnchor()
    local viewport = Cam.ViewportSize()
    local margin = 20

    if Config.StatsPosition == "TopRight" then
        return Vector2.new(viewport.X - margin, margin + 40), Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    elseif Config.StatsPosition == "BottomLeft" then
        return Vector2.new(margin, viewport.Y - margin - 90), Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    elseif Config.StatsPosition == "BottomRight" then
        return Vector2.new(viewport.X - margin, viewport.Y - margin - 90), Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    end

    return Vector2.new(margin, margin + 40), Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
end

function Hud.Update()
    if not Draw.Available then
        return
    end

    local viewport = Cam.ViewportSize()

    if Hud.Watermark then
        Hud.Watermark.Visible = Config.Watermark
        if Config.Watermark then
            Hud.Watermark.Text = Config.WatermarkText
                .. "  |  " .. string.format("%d fps", math.floor(State.FPS + 0.5))
            Hud.Watermark.Position = Vector2.new(viewport.X / 2 - 110, 12)
            Hud.Watermark.Color = Config.AccentColor
            Hud.Watermark.Transparency = 0.05
        end
    end

    if Hud.Stats then
        Hud.Stats.Visible = Config.StatsOverlay
        if Config.StatsOverlay then
            local position, alignment = Hud.StatsAnchor()

            local lines = {
                "players  " .. tostring(#Players:GetPlayers()),
                "targets  " .. tostring(#Cache.Get()),
                "fps      " .. string.format("%d", math.floor(State.FPS + 0.5)),
                "ping     " .. string.format("%d ms", math.floor(LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() * 1000 + 0.5)),
                "session  " .. string.format("%d s", math.floor(os.clock() - State.StartedAt)),
            }

            if State.Target then
                table.insert(lines, "locked   " .. State.Target.Name)
            end

            Hud.Stats.Text = table.concat(lines, "\n")
            Hud.Stats.Position = position
            Hud.Stats.Color = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 240)
            Hud.Stats.Transparency = 0.15
            Hud.Stats.Size = 14
        end
    end

    for _, line in ipairs(Hud.Lines) do
        Draw.Show(line, false)
    end

    if Hud.Keybinds then
        Hud.Keybinds.Visible = Config.KeybindList
        if Config.KeybindList then
            local rows = {}

            if Config.RageEnabled then
                table.insert(rows, (Config.RageRequireKey and Config.AimKeybind or "RAGE") .. "  rage")
            elseif Config.AimEnabled then
                table.insert(rows, Config.AimKeybind .. "  aimlock")
            end
            if Config.TriggerEnabled then
                table.insert(rows, Config.TriggerKeybind .. "  triggerbot")
            end
            if Config.FlyEnabled then
                table.insert(rows, Config.FlyKeybind .. "  fly")
            end
            if Config.ClickTeleport then
                table.insert(rows, Config.ClickTeleportKey .. "  teleport")
            end

            Hud.Keybinds.Text = table.concat(rows, "\n")
            Hud.Keybinds.Position = Vector2.new(viewport.X - 20 - 160, viewport.Y / 2 - 60)
            Hud.Keybinds.Color = Config.AccentColor
            Hud.Keybinds.Transparency = 0.2
        end
    end
end

--==================================================
-- MOVEMENT
--==================================================

local Movement = {}

Movement.FlyBody = nil
Movement.FlyGyro = nil
Movement.FlyRoot = nil
Movement.FlyMode = nil
Movement.LastNoclip = 0
Movement.LastJump = 0

-- Original humanoid values, captured the first time a toggle is switched on.
-- Disabling a movement toggle has to put back what the game had, not a
-- hardcoded 16 studs: Rivals raises walk speed during a sprint and a hardcoded
-- restore yanks the player back to a crawl mid-fight.
Movement.Saved = {}

local function LocalCharacter()
    return PlayerUtil.GetCharacter(LocalPlayer)
end

local function LocalHumanoid()
    local character = LocalCharacter()
    if not character then
        return nil
    end
    return character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

-- A respawn can hand back a humanoid with different defaults, so the baseline
-- is dropped with the rig and recaptured by the next apply pass. The applied
-- flags are cleared too: the new rig has not been modified yet, so a disable
-- before the next apply must not try to restore a baseline that no longer
-- exists.
function Movement.ClearSaved()
    Movement.Saved = {}
    Movement.SpeedApplied = false
    Movement.JumpApplied = false
    Movement.GravityApplied = false
    Movement.HipApplied = false
end

local function SaveHumanoid()
    local humanoid = LocalHumanoid()
    if not humanoid then
        return nil
    end

    local saved = Movement.Saved
    if saved.WalkSpeed == nil then
        saved.WalkSpeed = humanoid.WalkSpeed
    end
    if saved.JumpPower == nil then
        saved.JumpPower = humanoid.JumpPower
    end
    if saved.JumpHeight == nil then
        saved.JumpHeight = humanoid.JumpHeight
    end
    if saved.UseJumpPower == nil then
        saved.UseJumpPower = humanoid.UseJumpPower
    end
    if saved.HipHeight == nil then
        saved.HipHeight = humanoid.HipHeight
    end
    return humanoid
end

function Movement.RestoreSpeed()
    local humanoid = LocalHumanoid()
    local value = Movement.Saved.WalkSpeed
    if humanoid and value ~= nil then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.WalkSpeed = value
        end)
    end
end

function Movement.RestoreJump()
    local humanoid = LocalHumanoid()
    local saved = Movement.Saved
    if not humanoid or saved.UseJumpPower == nil then
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        humanoid.UseJumpPower = saved.UseJumpPower
        humanoid.JumpPower = saved.JumpPower
        humanoid.JumpHeight = saved.JumpHeight
    end)
end

function Movement.RestoreGravity()
    local value = Movement.Saved.Gravity
    if value ~= nil then
        pcall(function()
            Workspace.Gravity = value
        end)
    end
end

function Movement.RestoreHipHeight()
    local humanoid = LocalHumanoid()
    local value = Movement.Saved.HipHeight
    if humanoid and value ~= nil then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.HipHeight = value
        end)
    end
end

function Movement.ApplySpeed()
    -- Restoration lives in the loop, not only in the UI callback, so a flag
    -- changed externally (debug handle, keybind, another script) cannot leave
    -- the boosted value stamped on the humanoid. Rivals rewrites WalkSpeed to
    -- its own base every frame, so the value is only guaranteed for the frame
    -- it is written; the hook approach that pinned it froze movement because
    -- the game's controller re-zeroes velocity on every detected change.
    if not Config.WalkSpeedEnabled then
        if Movement.SpeedApplied then
            Movement.RestoreSpeed()
            Movement.SpeedApplied = false
        end
        return
    end

    local humanoid = SaveHumanoid()
    if humanoid then
        Movement.SpeedApplied = true
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.WalkSpeed = Config.WalkSpeed
        end)
    end
end

function Movement.ApplyJump()
    -- Both toggles off means the feature is off, so put the original jump
    -- profile back. Returning early here was why turning Jump Height off left
    -- the character floating at the boosted value until respawn. The flag keeps
    -- the idle 0.2 s pass from re-stamping the baseline over the game's own
    -- jump changes once the restore has run.
    if not Config.JumpPowerEnabled and not Config.JumpHeightEnabled then
        if Movement.JumpApplied then
            Movement.RestoreJump()
            Movement.JumpApplied = false
        end
        return
    end

    local humanoid = SaveHumanoid()
    if not humanoid then
        return
    end

    Movement.JumpApplied = true

    if Config.JumpHeightEnabled then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.JumpHeight = Config.JumpHeight
            humanoid.UseJumpPower = false
        end)
    elseif Config.JumpPowerEnabled then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.JumpPower = Config.JumpPower
            humanoid.UseJumpPower = true
        end)
    end
end

function Movement.ApplyGravity()
    if not Config.GravityEnabled then
        if Movement.GravityApplied then
            Movement.RestoreGravity()
            Movement.GravityApplied = false
        end
        return
    end

    if Movement.Saved.Gravity == nil then
        Movement.Saved.Gravity = Workspace.Gravity
    end

    Movement.GravityApplied = true

    pcall(function()
        Workspace.Gravity = Config.Gravity
    end)
end

function Movement.StopFly()
    if Movement.FlyBody then
        pcall(function()
            Movement.FlyBody:Destroy()
        end)
        Movement.FlyBody = nil
    end
    if Movement.FlyGyro then
        pcall(function()
            Movement.FlyGyro:Destroy()
        end)
        Movement.FlyGyro = nil
    end
    Movement.FlyRoot = nil
    Movement.FlyMode = nil
    State.FlyAttachment = nil
end

function Movement.StartFly()
    local character = LocalCharacter()
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid = LocalHumanoid()

    if not root or not humanoid then
        return
    end

    Movement.StopFly()

    Movement.FlyMode = Config.FlyMode
    Movement.FlyRoot = root

    -- CFrame mode does not need movers. Keeping a BodyVelocity around while
    -- the root is written directly makes the two fight, and the physics step
    -- wins often enough that the character stutters instead of flying.
    if Config.FlyMode == "CFrame" then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.PlatformStand = true
        end)
        return
    end

    local body = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
    body.Name = "RIVALS_Fly"
    body.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e5, 1e5, 1e5)
    body.P = 2000
    body.Velocity = Vector3.zero
    body.Parent = root

    local gyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
    gyro.Name = "RIVALS_FlyGyro"
    gyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e5, 1e5, 1e5)
    gyro.P = 3000
    gyro.D = 50
    gyro.CFrame = root.CFrame
    gyro.Parent = root

    Movement.FlyBody = body
    Movement.FlyGyro = gyro

    pcall(function()
        humanoid.PlatformStand = true
    end)
end

-- Heartbeat is the only fly driver. The old build also had a RenderStepped
-- loop that wrote Vector3.zero into FlyBody.Velocity every frame, which landed
-- after this function and pinned the character in mid-air: fly looked enabled
-- and never moved. All movement now happens here, once per physics frame.
function Movement.StepFly(delta)
    if not Config.FlyEnabled then
        -- The UI toggle and keybind call StopFly themselves, but config can
        -- also be flipped by a debug handle or by another script. Clean up
        -- here too, otherwise PlatformStand stays on and the character is
        -- left floating with normal walking disabled.
        if Movement.FlyRoot or Movement.FlyBody or Movement.FlyGyro then
            Movement.StopFly()
            local humanoid = LocalHumanoid()
            if humanoid then
                pcall(function()
                    humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                end)
            end
        end
        return
    end

    local character = LocalCharacter()
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid = LocalHumanoid()
    if not root or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
        if Movement.FlyRoot or Movement.FlyBody or Movement.FlyGyro then
            Movement.StopFly()
        end
        return
    end

    local mode = Config.FlyMode
    local stale = Movement.FlyRoot ~= root or Movement.FlyMode ~= mode

    if mode == "Velocity" then
        stale = stale
            or not Movement.FlyBody
            or Movement.FlyBody.Parent ~= root
            or not Movement.FlyGyro
            or Movement.FlyGyro.Parent ~= root
    end

    if stale then
        Movement.StartFly()
        if mode == "Velocity" and not Movement.FlyBody then
            return
        end
    end

    pcall(function()
        humanoid.PlatformStand = true
    end)

    local camera = Cam.Get()
    local look = camera and camera.CFrame.LookVector or root.CFrame.LookVector
    local right = camera and camera.CFrame.RightVector or root.CFrame.RightVector

    local direction = Vector3.zero

    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
        direction = direction + look
    end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
        direction = direction - look
    end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
        direction = direction + right
    end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
        direction = direction - right
    end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
        direction = direction + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
    end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then
        direction = direction - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
    end

    if direction.Magnitude > 0 then
        direction = direction.Unit
    end

    local speed = Config.FlySpeed
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then
        speed = speed * 2
    end

    if mode == "CFrame" then
        pcall(function()
            local move = direction * speed * delta
            if move.Magnitude > 0 then
                local rotation = camera
                    and (camera.CFrame - camera.CFrame.Position)
                    or (root.CFrame - root.CFrame.Position)
                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position + move) * rotation
            end
            -- Cancels gravity and residual knockback while the root is being
            -- written; without it the character sinks between frames.
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
        return
    end

    if Movement.FlyBody then
        Movement.FlyBody.Velocity = direction * speed
    end
    if Movement.FlyGyro then
        local rotation = camera
            and (camera.CFrame - camera.CFrame.Position)
            or (root.CFrame - root.CFrame.Position)
        Movement.FlyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position) * rotation
    end
end

function Movement.ResetPhysics()
    Movement.StopFly()

    local humanoid = LocalHumanoid()
    if humanoid then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.PlatformStand = false
        end)
    end
end

function Movement.UpdateNoclip()
    if not Config.NoClip then
        return
    end

    if os.clock() - Movement.LastNoclip < 0.15 then
        return
    end
    Movement.LastNoclip = os.clock()

    local character = LocalCharacter()
    if not character then
        return
    end

    for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanCollide then
            pcall(function()
                part.CanCollide = false
            end)
        end
    end
end

function Movement.UpdateSpin(delta)
    if not Config.SpinEnabled then
        return
    end

    local root = LocalCharacter() and LocalCharacter():FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then
        return
    end

    local increment = math.rad(Config.SpinSpeed) * delta
    pcall(function()
        root.CFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, increment, 0)
    end)
end

function Movement.UpdateHipHeight()
    if not Config.HipHeightEnabled then
        if Movement.HipApplied then
            Movement.RestoreHipHeight()
            Movement.HipApplied = false
        end
        return
    end

    local humanoid = SaveHumanoid()
    if humanoid then
        Movement.HipApplied = true
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.HipHeight = Config.HipHeight
        end)
    end
end

function Movement.TeleportTo(position, part)
    local root = part or (LocalCharacter() and LocalCharacter():FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
    if not root then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
    end)
end

function Movement.TeleportToPlayer(player)
    local targetRoot = PlayerUtil.GetRoot(player)
    if not targetRoot then
        return
    end

    local myRoot = PlayerUtil.GetRoot(LocalPlayer)
    if not myRoot then
        return
    end

    local offset = (myRoot.Position - targetRoot.Position).Magnitude

    if offset > 200 then
        pcall(function()
            myRoot.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
        end)
    end
end

function Movement.ResetCharacter()
    local character = LocalCharacter()
    if not character then
        return
    end

    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.Health = 0
        end)
    end
end

_G.RIVALS_MOVEMENT = Movement

--==================================================
-- CLICK TELEPORT
--==================================================

local ClickTeleport = {}

ClickTeleport.Enabled = false

function ClickTeleport.Update(mouseLocation)
    if not Config.ClickTeleport then
        return
    end

    if not ClickTeleport.Enabled then
        return
    end

    local camera = Camera
    if not camera then
        return
    end

    local unitRay = camera:ViewportPointToRay(mouseLocation.X, mouseLocation.Y)

    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.IgnoreWater = true

    local character = LocalCharacter()
    if character then
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { character }
    end

    local result = Workspace:Raycast(
        unitRay.Origin,
        unitRay.Direction * Config.ClickTeleportDistance,
        params
    )

    if result and result.Position then
        Movement.TeleportTo(result.Position)
    end

    ClickTeleport.Enabled = false
end

--==================================================
-- COMBAT EXTRAS
--==================================================

local Combat = {}

Combat.LastSound = 0
Combat.LastClick = false
Combat.LastAmmoCheck = 0
Combat.Markers = {}

function Combat.PlayHitSound()
    if not Config.HitSound then
        return
    end
    if os.clock() - Combat.LastSound < 0.04 then
        return
    end
    Combat.LastSound = os.clock()

    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.SoundId = Config.HitSoundId
    sound.Volume = Config.HitSoundVolume
    sound.Parent = SoundService
    sound:Play()

    task.delay(2, function()
        if sound then
            sound:Destroy()
        end
    end)
end

function Combat.ShowMarker()
    if not Config.HitMarker then
        return
    end
    State.MarkerUntil = os.clock() + 0.2
end

local function TargetUnderCrosshair()
    local center = Cam.ScreenCenter()

    for _, entry in ipairs(Cache.Get()) do
        if entry.Alive and not (Config.TriggerTeamCheck and entry.Teammate) then
            local part = PlayerUtil.GetPart(entry.Player, "Head")
            if part then
                local screen, onScreen = Cam.WorldToScreen(part.Position)
                if screen and onScreen then
                    if (screen - center).Magnitude <= math.max(Config.TriggerFOV, 14) then
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return false
end

function Combat.Update()
    local down = UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)

    if down and not Combat.LastClick then
