        State.LastFire = os.clock()

        if (Config.HitSound or Config.HitMarker) and TargetUnderCrosshair() then
            Combat.PlayHitSound()
            Combat.ShowMarker()
        end
    end

    Combat.LastClick = down

    if Config.AutoReload then
        if os.clock() - Combat.LastAmmoCheck > Config.AutoReloadDelay then
            Combat.LastAmmoCheck = os.clock()

            local character = LocalCharacter()
            local tool = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

            if tool then
                local ammo = tool:GetAttribute("Ammo")
                    or tool:GetAttribute("CurrentAmmo")
                    or tool:GetAttribute("AmmoInClip")

                if type(ammo) == "number" and ammo <= 0 then
                    pcall(function()
                        tool:Activate()
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    Combat.TrackDamage()
end

function Combat.InitMarker()
    if not Draw.Available then
        return
    end

    for index = 1, 4 do
        local line = Draw.New("Line")
        if line then
            line.Thickness = 2
        end
        Combat.Markers[index] = line
    end
end

-- Damage numbers are derived from health deltas, not from a damage feed. A
-- health drop inside a one second window after a local shot is treated as
-- damage this player caused; nothing else is claimed.
function Combat.InitDamage()
    if not Draw.Available then
        return
    end

    Combat.DamageTexts = {}

    for index = 1, 16 do
        local text = Draw.New("Text")
        if text then
            text.Center = true
            text.Outline = true
            text.Font = 2
            text.Size = 16
        end
        Combat.DamageTexts[index] = text
    end
end

function Combat.TrackDamage()
    if not Config.DamageNumbers then
        if #State.DamageNumbers > 0 then
            table.clear(State.DamageNumbers)
        end
        return
    end

    if os.clock() - (Combat.LastTrack or 0) < 0.05 then
        return
    end
    Combat.LastTrack = os.clock()

    for _, entry in ipairs(Cache.Get()) do
        local previous = State.HealthTrack[entry.Player]
        local current = entry.Health

        if previous and current < previous - 0.5 then
            if os.clock() - (State.LastFire or 0) < 1 then
                table.insert(State.DamageNumbers, {
                    Position = entry.Root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2.6, 0),
                    Amount = math.floor(previous - current + 0.5),
                    Born = os.clock(),
                })
            end
        end

        State.HealthTrack[entry.Player] = current
    end

    for player in pairs(State.HealthTrack) do
        if not player.Parent then
            State.HealthTrack[player] = nil
        end
    end

    while #State.DamageNumbers > 16 do
        table.remove(State.DamageNumbers, 1)
    end
end

function Combat.RenderDamage()
    if not Combat.DamageTexts then
        return
    end

    local now = os.clock()

    for index = #State.DamageNumbers, 1, -1 do
        local item = State.DamageNumbers[index]
        if now - item.Born > 1 then
            table.remove(State.DamageNumbers, index)
        end
    end

    for _, text in ipairs(Combat.DamageTexts) do
        Draw.Show(text, false)
    end

    local slot = 1

    for _, item in ipairs(State.DamageNumbers) do
        local text = Combat.DamageTexts[slot]
        if not text then
            break
        end

        local age = now - item.Born
        local screen, _, z = Cam.WorldToScreen(
            item.Position + Vector3.new(0, age * 1.8, 0)
        )

        if screen and z > 0 then
            text.Text = tostring(item.Amount)
            text.Position = screen
            text.Color = Config.DamageNumberColor
            text.Transparency = Util.Clamp(age, 0, 1)
            text.Size = 16
            text.Visible = true
            slot = slot + 1
        end
    end
end

function Combat.RenderMarker()
    local active = os.clock() < State.MarkerUntil

    for index, line in ipairs(Combat.Markers) do
        if active then
            local center = Cam.ScreenCenter()
            local spread = 10
            local length = 6

            local offsets = {
                { Vector2.new(-1, -1), 1 },
                { Vector2.new(1, -1), 2 },
                { Vector2.new(-1, 1), 3 },
                { Vector2.new(1, 1), 4 },
            }

            local offset = offsets[index]
            if offset then
                local direction = offset[1]
                local from = center + direction * spread
                local to = center + direction * (spread + length)

                line.From = from
                line.To = to
                line.Color = Config.HitMarkerColor
                line.Transparency = 0
                line.Visible = true
            end
        else
            line.Visible = false
        end
    end
end

--==================================================
-- PLAYER ACTIONS
--==================================================

local Actions = {}

function Actions.Spectate(player)
    if not player then
        return
    end

    if State.Spectating == player then
        State.Spectating = nil
        Camera.CameraSubject = PlayerUtil.GetHumanoid(LocalPlayer)
        return
    end

    local humanoid = PlayerUtil.GetHumanoid(player)
    if humanoid then
        State.Spectating = player
        pcall(function()
            Camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
        end)
    end
end

function Actions.ResetCamera()
    State.Spectating = nil
    local humanoid = PlayerUtil.GetHumanoid(LocalPlayer)
    if humanoid and Camera then
        pcall(function()
            Camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
        end)
    end
end

function Actions.Copy(text)
    if setclipboard then
        pcall(function()
            setclipboard(text)
        end)
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function Actions.Follow(player)
    if not player then
        return
    end

    local targetRoot = PlayerUtil.GetRoot(player)
    local myRoot = PlayerUtil.GetRoot(LocalPlayer)

    if not targetRoot or not myRoot then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        myRoot.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame
            * CFrame.new(0, 2, math.max(targetRoot.Size.Z, 3) + 2)
    end)
end

--==================================================
-- ANTI AFK
--==================================================

local AntiAFK = {}

AntiAFK.Connection = nil

function AntiAFK.Start()
    if AntiAFK.Connection then
        return
    end

    AntiAFK.Connection = LocalPlayer.Idled:Connect(function()
        if not Config.AntiAFK then
            return
        end
        pcall(function()
            local virtual = VirtualUser or game:GetService("VirtualUser")
            virtual:CaptureController()
            virtual:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
        end)
    end)
end

function AntiAFK.Stop()
    if AntiAFK.Connection then
        pcall(function()
            AntiAFK.Connection:Disconnect()
        end)
        AntiAFK.Connection = nil
    end
end

--==================================================
-- SKIN CHANGER
--==================================================

-- The supplied Aniha changer hooks two methods on the game's ClientViewModel:
-- GetWrap decides which wrap the viewmodel paints with, and new rewrites the
-- serialised skin name before the model is built. Everything else it shipped
-- (its own ScreenGui, the K toggle, the 1300px grid) belonged to a standalone
-- script, so only the hook logic and the persisted loadout survive here.
--
-- The weapon/skin table in the original was hand-maintained and had drifted:
-- names such as "10 Billion Visits" no longer exist and CosmeticLibrary.Equip
-- does not exist at all. The real library groups every cosmetic by ItemName,
-- so the lists are rebuilt from the live game at init and the hardcoded table
-- is only a fallback for the moment before the modules resolve.

local SkinChanger = {}

SkinChanger.Ready = false
SkinChanger.Hooked = false
SkinChanger.Initializing = false
SkinChanger.Library = nil
SkinChanger.ItemLibrary = nil
SkinChanger.ReplicatedClass = nil
SkinChanger.ClientViewModel = nil
SkinChanger.Originals = nil
SkinChanger.Viewmodels = {}
SkinChanger.OnRefresh = nil
SkinChanger.LastStatus = "Waiting for game modules"
SkinChanger.File = "RIVALS/Skins.json"

-- Fallback only. The live CosmeticLibrary is authoritative and is merged in

local WrapList = {
    "None", "Gold", "Diamond", "Midas Touch", "Community Wrap", "Blush Wrapping", "Brain", "Crystalliz",
    "Damascus", "Black Damascus", ".exe wrap", "Groove", "Hollow Wrap", "Hesper", "Hyperdrive",

    "Mainframe Wrap", "Honeycomb Wrap", "Black Opal Wrap", "Patriot", "PB&J Wrap", "Digital Camo",
    "Street Camo", "Ocean Camo", "Circuit", "Clouds", "Woven", "Ladybug",

SkinChanger.Skins = {}
SkinChanger.Wraps = {}
SkinChanger.WeaponList = {}
SkinChanger.Data = {}

for weapon in pairs(SkinLists) do
    SkinChanger.Data[weapon] = { Skin = "Default", Wrap = "None" }
end

-- Requiring a game module from an executor is the one dependency that is not
-- guaranteed: some identities need the call elevated, and a few builds only
-- expose require through getgenv(). Try the plain path first, then the
-- elevated one, and give up quietly so the rest of the hub still loads.
local function requireModule(module)
    if not module then
        return nil
    end

    if type(require) == "function" then
        local ok, result = pcall(require, module)
        if ok and result ~= nil then
            return result
        end
    end

    if type(getgenv) == "function" then
        local env = getgenv()
        if type(env) == "table" and type(env.require) == "function" then
            local ok, result = pcall(env.require, module)
            if ok and result ~= nil then
                return result
            end
        end
    end

    if type(setthreadidentity) == "function" and type(getthreadidentity) == "function" then
        local okOld, old = pcall(getthreadidentity)
        if okOld then
            pcall(setthreadidentity, 2)
            local ok, result = pcall(require, module)
            pcall(setthreadidentity, old)
            if ok and result ~= nil then
                return result
            end
        end
    end

    return nil
end

local function copyArray(source, skipDefault)
    local result = {}
    for _, value in ipairs(source) do
        if not (skipDefault and value == "Default") and value ~= "None" then
            table.insert(result, value)
        end
    end
    return result
end

-- Seed the UI before Init() has a chance to reach the game modules. The tab is
-- built in the next part, which runs before the initialise section, so an empty
-- WeaponList here would leave the dropdown with nothing to show.
do
    local skins = {}
    for weapon, list in pairs(SkinLists) do
        skins[weapon] = copyArray(list, true)
        table.sort(skins[weapon])
    end
    local wraps = copyArray(WrapList)
    table.sort(wraps)
    local weapons = {}
    for weapon in pairs(skins) do
        table.insert(weapons, weapon)
    end
    table.sort(weapons)

    SkinChanger.Skins = skins
    SkinChanger.Wraps = wraps
    SkinChanger.WeaponList = weapons
end

function SkinChanger.BuildLists()
    local skins = {}
    local wraps = {}
    local library = SkinChanger.Library

    if library and type(library.Cosmetics) == "table" then
        for name, cosmetic in pairs(library.Cosmetics) do
            if type(name) == "string" and type(cosmetic) == "table" then
                if cosmetic.Type == "Skin" then
                    local weapon = cosmetic.ItemName
                    if type(weapon) == "string" and weapon ~= "" then
                        skins[weapon] = skins[weapon] or {}
                        table.insert(skins[weapon], name)
                    end
                elseif cosmetic.Type == "Wrap" then
                    table.insert(wraps, name)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- Fill gaps from the fallback table only when the live library had no
    -- entry for that weapon. Names for weapons the live library covers are
    -- never mixed with the fallback, so a removed skin cannot reappear.
    for weapon, list in pairs(SkinLists) do
        if not skins[weapon] then
            skins[weapon] = copyArray(list, true)
        end
    end

    if #wraps == 0 then
        wraps = copyArray(WrapList)
    end

    for _, list in pairs(skins) do
        table.sort(list)
    end
    table.sort(wraps)

    SkinChanger.Skins = skins
    SkinChanger.Wraps = wraps

    local weapons = {}
    for weapon in pairs(skins) do
        table.insert(weapons, weapon)
        if not SkinChanger.Data[weapon] then
            SkinChanger.Data[weapon] = { Skin = "Default", Wrap = "None" }
        end
    end
    table.sort(weapons)
    SkinChanger.WeaponList = weapons

    if not SkinChanger.Data[Config.SkinWeapon] then
        Config.SkinWeapon = weapons[1] or "Assault Rifle"
    end
end

function SkinChanger.GetSkinList(weapon)
    local list = { "Default" }
    local skins = SkinChanger.Skins[weapon]
    if skins then
        for _, name in ipairs(skins) do
            table.insert(list, name)
        end
    end
    return list
end

function SkinChanger.GetWrapList()
    local list = { "None" }
    for _, name in ipairs(SkinChanger.Wraps) do
        table.insert(list, name)
    end
    return list
end

function SkinChanger.Contains(list, value)
    for _, item in ipairs(list) do
        if item == value then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

function SkinChanger.GetCosmetic(name, kind)
    local library = SkinChanger.Library
    local base = library and library.Cosmetics and library.Cosmetics[name]
    if type(base) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end

    local data = {}
    for key, value in pairs(base) do
        data[key] = value
    end
    data.Name = name
    data.Type = kind

    if name == "AKEY-47" then
        data.IsMythical = true
        data.BundlePath = "Bundles"
    elseif string.find(name, "Gingerbread", 1, true) then
        data.BundlePath = "Festive Skin Case"
    elseif name == "Evil Trident" or name == "Devil's Trident" then
        data.DisplayName = "Evil Trident"
    end

    return data
end

function SkinChanger.WeaponOf(clientItem)
    if type(clientItem) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end

    -- ClientItem is a ReplicatedClass instance, so the authoritative name is in
    -- its data table. Depending on the build it may also mirror it onto .Name or
    -- onto .Info.Name, so try each and only accept names the skin table knows.
    local candidates = {}

    local ok, value = pcall(function()
        return clientItem:Get("Name")
    end)
    if ok and type(value) == "string" then
        table.insert(candidates, value)
    end

    if type(clientItem.Name) == "string" then
        table.insert(candidates, clientItem.Name)
    end

    local info = clientItem.Info
    if type(info) == "table" and type(info.Name) == "string" then
        table.insert(candidates, info.Name)
    end

    for _, name in ipairs(candidates) do
        if SkinChanger.Data[name] then
            return name
        end
    end

    return nil
end

function SkinChanger.Save()
    if type(writefile) ~= "function" then
        return false
    end
    local payload = {}
    for weapon, entry in pairs(SkinChanger.Data) do
        if entry.Skin ~= "Default" or entry.Wrap ~= "None" then
            payload[weapon] = { Skin = entry.Skin, Wrap = entry.Wrap }
        end
    end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        writefile(SkinChanger.File, HttpService:JSONEncode(payload))
    end)
    return ok
end

function SkinChanger.Load()
    if type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(readfile) ~= "function" then
        return false
    end

    local ok, raw = pcall(function()
        if isfile(SkinChanger.File) then
            return readfile(SkinChanger.File)
        end
        return nil
    end)
    if not ok or type(raw) ~= "string" or raw == "" then
        return false
    end

    local decodedOk, decoded = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw)
    end)
    if not decodedOk or type(decoded) ~= "table" then
        return false
    end

    local applied = 0
    for weapon, saved in pairs(decoded) do
        local entry = SkinChanger.Data[weapon]
        if entry and type(saved) == "table" then
            local skin = saved.Skin
            if type(skin) == "string" and SkinChanger.Contains(SkinChanger.GetSkinList(weapon), skin) then
                entry.Skin = skin
            end
            local wrap = saved.Wrap
            if type(wrap) == "string" and SkinChanger.Contains(SkinChanger.GetWrapList(), wrap) then
                entry.Wrap = wrap
            end
            applied = applied + 1
        end
    end

    SkinChanger.LastStatus = string.format("Loaded %d saved loadout%s", applied, applied == 1 and "" or "s")
    return applied > 0
end

function SkinChanger.Track(viewmodel)
    if not viewmodel then
        return
    end
    for _, existing in ipairs(SkinChanger.Viewmodels) do
        if existing == viewmodel then
            return
        end
    end
    table.insert(SkinChanger.Viewmodels, viewmodel)
end

function SkinChanger.RefreshWraps()
    local kept = {}
    for _, viewmodel in ipairs(SkinChanger.Viewmodels) do
        if type(viewmodel) == "table" and not viewmodel._destroyed then
            table.insert(kept, viewmodel)
            pcall(function()
                if viewmodel._UpdateWrap then
                    viewmodel:_UpdateWrap()
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    SkinChanger.Viewmodels = kept
end

function SkinChanger.SetSkin(weapon, skin)
    local entry = SkinChanger.Data[weapon]
    if not entry then
        SkinChanger.LastStatus = "Pick a weapon first"
        return false
    end
    if not SkinChanger.Contains(SkinChanger.GetSkinList(weapon), skin) then
        SkinChanger.LastStatus = "Skin not available: " .. tostring(skin)
        return false
    end

    entry.Skin = skin
    SkinChanger.LastStatus = weapon .. " -> " .. skin .. " (applies on next equip)"
    SkinChanger.Save()
    SkinChanger.RefreshWraps()
    return true
end

function SkinChanger.SetWrap(weapon, wrap)
    local entry = SkinChanger.Data[weapon]
    if not entry then
        SkinChanger.LastStatus = "Pick a weapon first"
        return false
    end
    if not SkinChanger.Contains(SkinChanger.GetWrapList(), wrap) then
        SkinChanger.LastStatus = "Wrap not available: " .. tostring(wrap)
        return false
    end

    entry.Wrap = wrap
    SkinChanger.LastStatus = weapon .. " -> wrap " .. wrap
    SkinChanger.Save()
    SkinChanger.RefreshWraps()
    return true
end

function SkinChanger.Reset()
    for _, entry in pairs(SkinChanger.Data) do
        entry.Skin = "Default"
        entry.Wrap = "None"
    end
    SkinChanger.LastStatus = "All skins and wraps reset"
    SkinChanger.Save()
    SkinChanger.RefreshWraps()
end

function SkinChanger.StatusText()
    if not SkinChanger.Ready then
        return SkinChanger.LastStatus or "Waiting for game modules"
    end

    local overrides = 0
    for _, entry in pairs(SkinChanger.Data) do
        if entry.Skin ~= "Default" or entry.Wrap ~= "None" then
            overrides = overrides + 1
        end
    end

    return string.format(
        "%d weapons / %d wraps / %d saved overrides",
        #SkinChanger.WeaponList,
        #SkinChanger.Wraps,
        overrides
    )
end

function SkinChanger.InstallHooks()
    if SkinChanger.Hooked then
        return
    end

    local clientViewModel = SkinChanger.ClientViewModel
    if type(clientViewModel) ~= "table" then
        return
    end

    local store = rawget(_G, "RIVALS_SKIN_ORIGINALS")
    if type(store) ~= "table" then
        store = {}
        _G.RIVALS_SKIN_ORIGINALS = store
    end

    -- A reload must not wrap the wrapper. Luau functions are not tables, so a
    -- marker field cannot be attached to them; identify the wrapper we
    -- installed last run by identity and put the original back.
    local currentNew = clientViewModel.new
    if type(currentNew) == "function" and store.WrapperNew and currentNew == store.WrapperNew then
        clientViewModel.new = store.New or currentNew
        currentNew = clientViewModel.new
    end
    local currentWrap = clientViewModel.GetWrap
    if type(currentWrap) == "function" and store.WrapperWrap and currentWrap == store.WrapperWrap then
        clientViewModel.GetWrap = store.GetWrap or currentWrap
        currentWrap = clientViewModel.GetWrap
    end

    if type(currentNew) ~= "function" or type(currentWrap) ~= "function" then
        return
    end

    store.New = currentNew
    store.GetWrap = currentWrap

    SkinChanger.Originals = {
        New = currentNew,
        GetWrap = currentWrap,
    }

    local originalNew = currentNew
    local originalWrap = currentWrap

    local function hookedNew(replicatedData, clientItem)
        if Config.SkinsEnabled and type(replicatedData) == "table" then
            pcall(function()
                local weapon = SkinChanger.WeaponOf(clientItem)
                local entry = weapon and SkinChanger.Data[weapon]
                if not entry or entry.Skin == "Default" then
                    return
                end

                local fighter = rawget(clientItem, "ClientFighter") or clientItem.ClientFighter
                if fighter and fighter.Player and fighter.Player ~= LocalPlayer then
                    return
                end

                local cosmetic = SkinChanger.GetCosmetic(entry.Skin, "Skin")
                if not cosmetic then
                    return
                end

                local dataKey = SkinChanger.ReplicatedClass:ToEnum("Data")
                local skinKey = SkinChanger.ReplicatedClass:ToEnum("Skin")
                local nameKey = SkinChanger.ReplicatedClass:ToEnum("Name")
                if not dataKey or not skinKey or not nameKey then
                    return
                end

                replicatedData[dataKey] = replicatedData[dataKey] or {}
                replicatedData[dataKey][skinKey] = cosmetic
                replicatedData[dataKey][nameKey] = entry.Skin
            end)
        end

        local viewmodel = originalNew(replicatedData, clientItem)
        SkinChanger.Track(viewmodel)

        -- Wraps are painted in _Init, but the viewmodel's arms are rebuilt a
        -- beat later. One deferred pass makes a saved wrap visible on equip.
        if Config.SkinsEnabled and viewmodel then
            task.delay(0.1, function()
                pcall(function()
                    if viewmodel and not viewmodel._destroyed and viewmodel._UpdateWrap then
                        viewmodel:_UpdateWrap()
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end

        return viewmodel
    end
    store.WrapperNew = hookedNew

    local function hookedGetWrap(self)
        if Config.SkinsEnabled and type(self) == "table" then
            local ok, result = pcall(function()
                local weapon = SkinChanger.WeaponOf(self.ClientItem)
                local entry = weapon and SkinChanger.Data[weapon]
                if entry and entry.Wrap ~= "None" then
                    return SkinChanger.GetCosmetic(entry.Wrap, "Wrap")
                end
                return nil
            end)
            if ok and result then
                return result
            end
        end
        return originalWrap(self)
    end
    store.WrapperWrap = hookedGetWrap

    clientViewModel.new = hookedNew
    clientViewModel.GetWrap = hookedGetWrap
    SkinChanger.Originals.WrapperNew = hookedNew
    SkinChanger.Originals.WrapperWrap = hookedGetWrap
    SkinChanger.Hooked = true
end

function SkinChanger.Init()
    if SkinChanger.Ready then
        return true
    end
    if SkinChanger.Initializing then
        return false
    end
    SkinChanger.Initializing = true

    local ok = pcall(function()
        local modules = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
        if not modules then
            return
        end

        local library = requireModule(modules:FindFirstChild("CosmeticLibrary"))
        local itemLibrary = requireModule(modules:FindFirstChild("ItemLibrary"))
        local replicatedClass = requireModule(modules:FindFirstChild("ReplicatedClass"))

        local playerScripts = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
        local clientModules = playerScripts and playerScripts:FindFirstChild("Modules")
        local classes = clientModules and clientModules:FindFirstChild("ClientReplicatedClasses")
        local fighter = classes and classes:FindFirstChild("ClientFighter")
        local clientItem = fighter and fighter:FindFirstChild("ClientItem")
        local clientViewModel = clientItem and clientItem:FindFirstChild("ClientViewModel")

        local viewmodelModule = requireModule(clientViewModel)

        if not library or not itemLibrary or not replicatedClass or not viewmodelModule then
            SkinChanger.LastStatus = "Game modules not ready"
            return
        end

        SkinChanger.Library = library
        SkinChanger.ItemLibrary = itemLibrary
        SkinChanger.ReplicatedClass = replicatedClass
        SkinChanger.ClientViewModel = viewmodelModule

        SkinChanger.BuildLists()
        SkinChanger.Load()
        SkinChanger.InstallHooks()

        SkinChanger.Ready = true
        SkinChanger.LastStatus = "Ready - " .. SkinChanger.StatusText()

        if type(SkinChanger.OnRefresh) == "function" then
            pcall(SkinChanger.OnRefresh)
        end
    end)

    SkinChanger.Initializing = false

    if not ok then
        SkinChanger.LastStatus = "Init failed - retrying"
    end
    return SkinChanger.Ready
end

function SkinChanger.Unload()
    local clientViewModel = SkinChanger.ClientViewModel
    local originals = SkinChanger.Originals

    if clientViewModel and originals then
        pcall(function()
            if originals.WrapperNew and clientViewModel.new == originals.WrapperNew then
                clientViewModel.new = originals.New
            end
            if originals.WrapperWrap and clientViewModel.GetWrap == originals.WrapperWrap then
                clientViewModel.GetWrap = originals.GetWrap
            end
        end)
    end

    SkinChanger.Hooked = false
    SkinChanger.Ready = false
    SkinChanger.Viewmodels = {}
end

_G.RIVALS_SKIN = SkinChanger

--==================================================
-- KEY CAPTURE
--==================================================

-- One listener for the whole hub. A key picker sets Listening to a function;
-- the next press is consumed by it and goes nowhere else. Every runtime
-- feature reads its key out of Config, so a rebind takes effect on the next
-- input event with no rewiring.
local KeyCapture = {}

KeyCapture.Listening = nil

function KeyCapture.Name(input)
    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
        return input.UserInputType.Name
    end
    local code = input.KeyCode
    if code == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
        return nil
    end
    return code.Name
end

function KeyCapture.Begin(callback)
    KeyCapture.Listening = callback
end

function KeyCapture.Cancel()
    KeyCapture.Listening = nil
end

--==================================================
-- SKIN CHANGER TAB
--==================================================

do
    local Page = Tabs.SkinChanger

    local SkinBox = Page:AddLeftGroupbox({ Name = "Weapon Skin" })
    local WrapBox = Page:AddRightGroupbox({ Name = "Weapon Wrap" })

    local WeaponDropdown
    local SkinDropdown
    local WrapDropdown

    local function currentSkin()
        local entry = SkinChanger.Data[Config.SkinWeapon]
        return entry and entry.Skin or "Default"
    end

    local function currentWrap()
        local entry = SkinChanger.Data[Config.SkinWeapon]
        return entry and entry.Wrap or "None"
    end

    local function refreshSkinDropdown()
        local list = SkinChanger.GetSkinList(Config.SkinWeapon)
        SkinDropdown:Refresh(list, false, true)
        local value = currentSkin()
        if not SkinChanger.Contains(list, value) then
            value = "Default"
        end
        SkinDropdown:Set(value, true)
    end

    local function refreshWrapDropdown()
        local list = SkinChanger.GetWrapList()
        WrapDropdown:Refresh(list, false, true)
        local value = currentWrap()
        if not SkinChanger.Contains(list, value) then
            value = "None"
        end
        WrapDropdown:Set(value, true)
    end

    if not SkinChanger.Contains(SkinChanger.WeaponList, Config.SkinWeapon) then
        Config.SkinWeapon = SkinChanger.WeaponList[1] or "Assault Rifle"
    end

    SkinBox:Toggle({
        Name = "Enabled",
        Description = "Master switch for the cosmetic hooks",
        Default = Config.SkinsEnabled,
        Flag = "SkinsEnabled",
        Callback = function(value)
            Config.SkinsEnabled = value
            if value then
                SkinChanger.RefreshWraps()
            else
                SkinChanger.RefreshWraps()
            end
        end,
    })

    WeaponDropdown = SkinBox:Dropdown({
        Name = "Weapon",
        Description = "Loadout slot whose skin and wrap are replaced",
        Options = SkinChanger.WeaponList,
        Default = Config.SkinWeapon,
        Callback = function(value)
            if type(value) ~= "string" or not SkinChanger.Data[value] then
                return
            end
            Config.SkinWeapon = value
            refreshSkinDropdown()
            refreshWrapDropdown()
        end,
    })

    SkinDropdown = SkinBox:Dropdown({
        Name = "Skin",
        Description = "Rebuilt from the live CosmeticLibrary",
        Options = SkinChanger.GetSkinList(Config.SkinWeapon),
        Default = currentSkin(),
        Callback = function(value)
            SkinChanger.SetSkin(Config.SkinWeapon, value)
        end,
    })

    SkinBox:Button({
        Name = "Apply Skin",
        Icon = "check",
        Callback = function()
            SkinChanger.Save()
            SkinChanger.RefreshWraps()
            Notify("SKIN CHANGER", Config.SkinWeapon .. " set to " .. currentSkin() .. ". Re-equip the weapon to rebuild the viewmodel.", "shirt", 4)
        end,
    })

    SkinBox:Paragraph({
        Title = "How skins apply",
        Content = "Rivals builds a ClientViewModel when a weapon is equipped, so a new skin lands on the next equip of that weapon. Pull it out and back, or swap weapons once. The selection is saved immediately.",
    })

    WrapDropdown = WrapBox:Dropdown({
        Name = "Wrap",
        Description = "Painted by the game's own WrapController",
        Options = SkinChanger.GetWrapList(),
        Default = currentWrap(),
        Callback = function(value)
            SkinChanger.SetWrap(Config.SkinWeapon, value)
        end,
    })

    WrapBox:Button({
        Name = "Apply Wrap",
        Icon = "check",
        Callback = function()
            SkinChanger.Save()
            SkinChanger.RefreshWraps()
            Notify("SKIN CHANGER", Config.SkinWeapon .. " wrap: " .. currentWrap(), "paintbrush", 3)
        end,
    })

    WrapBox:Divider()

    WrapBox:Button({
        Name = "Save Loadout",
        Icon = "save",
        Callback = function()
            if SkinChanger.Save() then
                Notify("SKIN CHANGER", "Loadout written to " .. SkinChanger.File, "save", 3)
            else
                Notify("SKIN CHANGER", "Could not write the loadout file.", "triangle-alert", 3)
            end
        end,
    })

    WrapBox:Button({
        Name = "Load Loadout",
        Icon = "folder-open",
        Callback = function()
            if SkinChanger.Load() then
                refreshSkinDropdown()
                refreshWrapDropdown()
                SkinChanger.RefreshWraps()
                Notify("SKIN CHANGER", SkinChanger.LastStatus, "folder-open", 3)
            else
                Notify("SKIN CHANGER", "No saved loadout found.", "triangle-alert", 3)
            end
        end,
    })

    WrapBox:Button({
        Name = "Reset All",
        Icon = "rotate-ccw",
        Callback = function()
            SkinChanger.Reset()
            refreshSkinDropdown()
            refreshWrapDropdown()
            Notify("SKIN CHANGER", "Every skin and wrap reset to the game default.", "rotate-ccw", 3)
        end,
    })

    WrapBox:Divider()

    WrapBox:Label({
        Text = SkinChanger.StatusText(),
        UpdateRate = 1,
        Update = function()
            return SkinChanger.StatusText()
        end,
    })

    WrapBox:Label({
        Text = "File: " .. SkinChanger.File,
    })

    SkinChanger.OnRefresh = function()
        if not SkinChanger.Contains(SkinChanger.WeaponList, Config.SkinWeapon) then
            Config.SkinWeapon = SkinChanger.WeaponList[1] or "Assault Rifle"
        end
        WeaponDropdown:Refresh(SkinChanger.WeaponList, false, true)
        WeaponDropdown:Set(Config.SkinWeapon, true)
        refreshSkinDropdown()
        refreshWrapDropdown()
    end

    refreshSkinDropdown()
    refreshWrapDropdown()
end

            Config.AutoReload = value
        end,
    })

    WeaponBox:Slider({
        Name = "Reload Check Rate",
        Min = 0.05,
        Max = 1,
        Step = 0.05,
        Default = Config.AutoReloadDelay,
        Flag = "AutoReloadDelay",
        Callback = function(value)
            Config.AutoReloadDelay = value
        end,
    })

    WeaponBox:Divider()

    WeaponBox:Label({
        Text = "Equipped: -",
        UpdateRate = 0.3,
        Update = function()
            local character = PlayerUtil.GetCharacter(LocalPlayer)
            local tool = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
            if not tool then
                return "Equipped: -"
            end

            local ammo = tool:GetAttribute("Ammo")
                or tool:GetAttribute("CurrentAmmo")
                or tool:GetAttribute("AmmoInClip")

            if type(ammo) == "number" then
                return string.format("Equipped: %s  (%d)", tool.Name, math.floor(ammo))
            end

            return "Equipped: " .. tool.Name
        end,
    })

    WeaponBox:Paragraph({
        Title = "Attributes",
        Content = "Reads Ammo, CurrentAmmo or AmmoInClip off the tool. If a game keeps its magazine in a remote or a UI value, Auto Reload has nothing to read and does nothing.",
    })

    local WorldPage = Tabs.Combat:SubTab({ Name = "Reach" })

    local ReachBox = WorldPage:AddLeftGroupbox({ Name = "Melee Reach" })
    local HitboxLink = WorldPage:AddRightGroupbox({ Name = "Hitbox Link" })

    ReachBox:Paragraph({
        Title = "How reach works",
        Content = "Rivals resolves a hit against its own rig: HitboxHead (IsCritical) and HitboxBody, with HitboxHeadSmall / HitboxBodySmall as the IsSmallHitbox variant and PhysicalHitboxHead as the only collidable one. All of it is server-side. What the client can change is which part the aim resolves to and what it draws, and that lives in Aim > Hitbox.",
    })

    ReachBox:Button({
        Name = "Open Hitbox Settings",
        Icon = "move-3d",
        Callback = function()
            Config.HitboxEnabled = true
            Notify("HITBOX", "Hitbox expander enabled. Tune it in Aim > Hitbox.", "move-3d", 3)
        end,
    })

    HitboxLink:Label({
        Text = "Active hitboxes: 0",
        UpdateRate = 0.5,
        Update = function()
            local count = 0
            for _ in pairs(State.Hitboxes) do
                count = count + 1
            end
            return "Active hitboxes: " .. tostring(count)
        end,
    })

    HitboxLink:Button({
        Name = "Clear Hitboxes",
        Icon = "trash-2",
        Callback = function()
            Config.HitboxEnabled = false
            Hitbox.Clear()
        end,
    })
end

            State.Coordinates = value
        end,
    })

    TeleportBox:Button({
        Name = "Teleport To Coordinates",
        Icon = "move-3d",
        Callback = function()
            local raw = State.Coordinates or "0, 50, 0"
            local x, y, z = string.match(raw, "([%-%d%.]+)%s*,%s*([%-%d%.]+)%s*,%s*([%-%d%.]+)")

            if not x then
                Notify("TELEPORT", "Could not read those coordinates.", "alert-triangle", 3)
                return
            end

            Movement.TeleportTo(Vector3.new(tonumber(x), tonumber(y), tonumber(z)))
        end,
    })

    TeleportBox:Button({
        Name = "Save Current Position",
        Icon = "map-pin",
        Callback = function()
            local root = PlayerUtil.GetRoot(LocalPlayer)
            if not root then
                return
            end
            local position = root.Position
            State.Coordinates = string.format("%.1f, %.1f, %.1f", position.X, position.Y, position.Z)
            Notify("TELEPORT", "Position saved: " .. State.Coordinates, "map-pin", 3)
        end,
    })

    local ServerPage = Tabs.Local:SubTab({ Name = "Server" })

    local ServerBox = ServerPage:AddLeftGroupbox({ Name = "Session" })

            local id = game.JobId
            if not id or id == "" then
                return "Job id: reserved server"
            end
            return "Job id: " .. string.sub(id, 1, 14) .. "..."
        end,
    })

    ServerBox:Button({
        Name = "Copy Job Id",
        Icon = "copy",
        Callback = function()
            if Actions.Copy(game.JobId) then
                Notify("SERVER", "Job id copied.", "copy", 2)
            end
        end,
    })

    ServerBox:Button({
        Name = "Rejoin",
        Icon = "refresh-cw",
        Callback = function()
            pcall(function()
                TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(
                    game.PlaceId,
                    game.JobId,
                    LocalPlayer
                )
            end)
        end,
    })

    ServerBox:Button({
        Name = "Server Hop",
        Icon = "shuffle",
        Callback = function()
            Notify("SERVER", "Rejoining a fresh instance.", "shuffle", 3)

            local ok = pcall(function()
                local url = string.format(
                    "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100",
                    game.PlaceId
                )

                local response = game:HttpGet(url)
                local decoded = HttpService:JSONDecode(response)
                local servers = decoded and decoded.data

                if not servers then
                    return
                end

                for _, server in ipairs(servers) do
                    if server.id ~= game.JobId and server.playing < server.maxPlayers then
                        TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, server.id, LocalPlayer)
                        return
                    end
                end
            end)

            if not ok then
                pcall(function()
                    TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId)
                end)
            end
        end,
    })

    MiscBox:Button({
        Name = "Reset Character",
        Icon = "skull",

            Movement.ResetCharacter()
        end,
    })

        Name = "Reset Camera",
        Icon = "camera",
        Callback = function()
            Actions.ResetCamera()
        end,
    })

    MiscBox:Button({
        Name = "Clear Hitboxes",
        Icon = "trash-2",

            Hitbox.Clear()
        end,
    })

    MiscBox:Divider()

    MiscBox:Paragraph({
        Title = "Rejoin",
        Content = "Rejoin puts you back in the same job. Server Hop walks the public server list and takes the first instance with a free slot.",
    })

--==================================================
-- PLAYERS TAB
--==================================================

-- The list is rebuilt, not patched. A row is three buttons and a label, and
-- rebuilding twelve rows costs less than keeping a diff engine correct across
-- join, leave, respawn and rename. Elements are destroyed through the UI's own
-- Destroy so their flags and listeners go with them.
local PlayerList = {}

PlayerList.Box = nil
PlayerList.Rows = {}
PlayerList.Filter = ""

function PlayerList.Rebuild()
    if not PlayerList.Box then
        return
    end

    for _, element in ipairs(PlayerList.Rows) do
        pcall(function()
            element:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    table.clear(PlayerList.Rows)

    local entries = {}

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer then
            local entry = Cache.Find(player)
            table.insert(entries, {
                Player = player,
                Entry = entry,
                Distance = entry and entry.Distance or math.huge,
            })
        end
    end

    table.sort(entries, function(a, b)
        return a.Distance < b.Distance
    end)

    local shown = 0

    for _, item in ipairs(entries) do
        local player = item.Player
        local name = PlayerUtil.GetDisplayName(player)

        local matches = PlayerList.Filter == ""
            or string.find(string.lower(name), string.lower(PlayerList.Filter), 1, true) ~= nil
            or string.find(string.lower(player.Name), string.lower(PlayerList.Filter), 1, true) ~= nil

        if matches and shown < 14 then
            shown = shown + 1

            local label = PlayerList.Box:Label({
                Text = string.format(
                    "%s  (%s)",
                    name,
                    item.Distance < math.huge
                        and string.format("%d m", math.floor(item.Distance / 3 + 0.5))
                        or "out of range"
                ),
            })
            table.insert(PlayerList.Rows, label)

            local spectate = PlayerList.Box:Button({
                Name = "Spectate  " .. name,
                Icon = "eye",
                Callback = function()
                    Actions.Spectate(player)
                end,
            })
            table.insert(PlayerList.Rows, spectate)

            local teleport = PlayerList.Box:Button({
                Name = "Teleport  " .. name,
                Icon = "move-3d",
                Callback = function()
                    Actions.Follow(player)
                    Notify("PLAYERS", "Moved to " .. name, "move-3d", 2)
                end,
            })
            table.insert(PlayerList.Rows, teleport)
        end
    end

    if shown == 0 then
        local empty = PlayerList.Box:Label({ Text = "No players match." })
        table.insert(PlayerList.Rows, empty)
    end
end

do
    local ListBox = Tabs.Players:AddLeftGroupbox({ Name = "Players" })
    local ActionBox = Tabs.Players:AddRightGroupbox({ Name = "Actions" })

    PlayerList.Box = ListBox

    ListBox:Input({
        Name = "Filter",
        Default = "",
        Placeholder = "name",
        Callback = function(value)
            PlayerList.Filter = value or ""
            task.defer(PlayerList.Rebuild)
        end,
    })

    ListBox:Button({
        Name = "Refresh List",
        Icon = "refresh-cw",
        Callback = function()
            Cache.Force()
            PlayerList.Rebuild()
        end,
    })

    ListBox:Label({
        Text = "Players: -",
        UpdateRate = 1,
        Update = function()
            return string.format("Players: %d", #Players:GetPlayers())
        end,
    })

    ActionBox:Button({
        Name = "Copy My Name",
        Icon = "copy",
        Callback = function()
            Actions.Copy(LocalPlayer.Name)
            Notify("CLIPBOARD", "Copied " .. LocalPlayer.Name, "copy", 2)
        end,
    })

    ActionBox:Button({
        Name = "Copy My User Id",
        Icon = "copy",
        Callback = function()
            Actions.Copy(tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId))
            Notify("CLIPBOARD", "Copied user id.", "copy", 2)
        end,
    })

    ActionBox:Divider()

    ActionBox:Button({
        Name = "Reset Camera",
        Icon = "camera",
        Callback = function()
            Actions.ResetCamera()
        end,
    })

    ActionBox:Button({
        Name = "Teleport To Nearest",
        Icon = "crosshair",
        Callback = function()
            Cache.Force()

            local best, bestDistance = nil, math.huge
            for _, entry in ipairs(Cache.Get()) do
                if entry.Alive and entry.Distance < bestDistance then
                    bestDistance = entry.Distance
                    best = entry
                end
            end

            if best then
                Actions.Follow(best.Player)
            end
        end,
    })

    ActionBox:Label({
        Text = "Targets: -",
        UpdateRate = 0.5,
        Update = function()
            local alive = 0
            for _, entry in ipairs(Cache.Get()) do
                if entry.Alive then
                    alive = alive + 1
                end
            end
            return string.format("Targets: %d", alive)
        end,
    })

    ActionBox:Paragraph({
        Title = "Rows",
        Content = "The list is sorted by distance and capped at fourteen rows. Filter rebuilds on the next frame; Refresh re-runs the player cache as well.",
    })

    task.defer(PlayerList.Rebuild)

    Connect(Players.PlayerAdded, function()
        task.defer(PlayerList.Rebuild)
    end)

    Connect(Players.PlayerRemoving, function()
        task.defer(PlayerList.Rebuild)
    end)
end

--==================================================
-- INPUT
--==================================================

-- Key capture runs first and swallows its press. Everything after it reads
-- Config, so a rebound key works on the same frame it is captured.
Connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gameProcessed)
    if KeyCapture.Listening then
        local name = KeyCapture.Name(input)
        if name then
            local callback = KeyCapture.Listening
            KeyCapture.Listening = nil
            callback(name)
        end
        return
    end

    if gameProcessed then
        return
    end

    local name = KeyCapture.Name(input)
    if not name then
        return
    end

    -- The aim key is read from live input state in Aim.Update, not here: the
    -- event never arrives when the game eats the press for ADS, and a toggle
    -- edge handled in two places flips twice per press.

    if name == Config.TriggerKeybind then
        Trigger.Held = true
    end

    if name == Config.FlyKeybind then
        Config.FlyEnabled = not Config.FlyEnabled
        if Config.FlyEnabled then
            Movement.StartFly()
        else
            Movement.StopFly()
            Movement.ResetPhysics()
        end
        Notify("FLY", Config.FlyEnabled and "Fly on" or "Fly off", "move-3d", 1)
    end

    if name == Config.ClickTeleportKey and Config.ClickTeleport then
        ClickTeleport.Enabled = true
    end
end)

Connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input, gameProcessed)
    if KeyCapture.Listening then
        return
    end

    local name = KeyCapture.Name(input)
    if not name then
        return
    end

    if name == Config.TriggerKeybind then
        Trigger.Held = false
    end
end)

Connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gameProcessed)
    if gameProcessed then
        return
    end

    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and ClickTeleport.Enabled then
        ClickTeleport.Update(UserInputService:GetMouseLocation())
    end
end)

-- Infinite jump is a state push, not a new jump. ChangeState(Jumping) is the
-- only client-side call that re-triggers the jump animation while airborne.
Connect(UserInputService.JumpRequest, function()
    if not Config.InfiniteJump then
        return
    end

    local character = PlayerUtil.GetCharacter(LocalPlayer)
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
        end)
    end
end)

-- Respawn is where every reference to the old rig goes stale. Drop the
-- per-character state instead of holding a dead part alive through a closure.
Connect(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function(character)
    Movement.ResetPhysics()
    Movement.ClearSaved()
    State.Target = nil
    State.TargetPart = nil

    task.delay(0.5, function()
        if Config.WalkSpeedEnabled then
            Movement.ApplySpeed()
        end
        if Config.JumpPowerEnabled or Config.JumpHeightEnabled then
            Movement.ApplyJump()
        end
        Movement.ApplyGravity()
    end)

    task.wait(1)
    if Config.FlyEnabled then
        Movement.StartFly()
    end
end)

--==================================================
-- INITIALISE DRAWING
--==================================================

AimVisual.Init()
Crosshair.Init()
Hud.Init()
Radar.Init()
Combat.InitMarker()
Combat.InitDamage()

if Config.AntiAFK then
    AntiAFK.Start()
end

Effects.ApplyAll()

-- The cosmetic hooks are optional. If the game modules are not up yet the
-- retry loop stays out of the frame path: the hub loads and runs either way,
-- and the tab explains that it is still waiting.
task.spawn(function()
    for _ = 1, 20 do
        if SkinChanger.Init() then
            return
        end
        task.wait(1)
    end
    warn("[RIVALS] Skin changer could not reach the cosmetic modules.")
    SkinChanger.LastStatus = "Unavailable - cosmetic modules not found"
end)

--==================================================
-- FRAME LOOP
--==================================================

local frameAccumulator = 0
local frameCounter = 0

local renderConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(delta)
    if Destroyed then
        return
    end

    State.FrameTime = delta
    State.FrameCount = (State.FrameCount or 0) + 1

    -- The camera instance is swapped on respawn in some games, and every
    -- projection below goes through it.
    Cam.Get()

    frameAccumulator = frameAccumulator + delta
    frameCounter = frameCounter + 1

    if frameAccumulator >= 0.25 then
        State.FPS = frameCounter / frameAccumulator
        frameAccumulator = 0
        frameCounter = 0
    end

    -- Visuals first. Anything that writes to the camera runs after them so a
    -- frame never renders with the old camera and the new boxes.
    ESP.Update()
    Radar.Update()
    Chams.Update()
    Crosshair.Update(delta)
    Combat.RenderMarker()
    Combat.RenderDamage()
    AimVisual.Update()
    Hud.Update()
    Viewmodel.Update()

    Aim.Update()
    Aim.Apply()
end)

table.insert(Connections, renderConnection)

-- The camera method has to run after the game's own camera code. Last.Value is
-- the last priority Roblox documents, and Rivals writes its Scriptable camera
-- there, so the assist sits above it: +1 is genuinely the final word on where
-- the view points this frame.
local AIM_RENDER_STEP = "RIVALS_AimCamera"

pcall(function()
    RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(AIM_RENDER_STEP)
end)

pcall(function()
    RunService:BindToRenderStep(AIM_RENDER_STEP, Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value + 1, function()
        if Destroyed then
            return
        end
        Aim.CameraApply()
    end)
end)

-- Heartbeat owns physics-adjacent work: it runs after the physics step, so a
-- bodymover written here is applied on the next step instead of being
-- overwritten by it.
local heartbeatAccumulator = 0

local heartbeatConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(delta)
    if Destroyed then
        return
    end

    heartbeatAccumulator = heartbeatAccumulator + delta

    Movement.StepFly(delta)
    Movement.UpdateSpin(delta)
    Movement.UpdateNoclip()

    if heartbeatAccumulator >= 0.2 then
        heartbeatAccumulator = 0

        Movement.ApplySpeed()
        Movement.ApplyJump()
        Movement.ApplyGravity()
        Movement.UpdateHipHeight()
        Hitbox.Update()

        if State.Spectating and not State.Spectating.Parent then
            Actions.ResetCamera()
        end
    end

    Trigger.Update()
    Combat.Update()
end)

table.insert(Connections, heartbeatConnection)

--==================================================
-- CLEANUP
--==================================================

local function Unload()
    if Destroyed then
        return
    end
    Destroyed = true

    DisconnectAll()

    pcall(function()
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RIVALS_AimCamera")
    end)

    AntiAFK.Stop()

    pcall(function()
        -- ResetPhysics also clears PlatformStand. Calling StopFly alone used
        -- to leave the character platform-standing after every reload.
        Movement.ResetPhysics()
    end)

    pcall(function()
        Hitbox.Clear()
    end)

    pcall(function()
        ESP.ClearAll()
    end)

    pcall(function()
        Chams.Clear()
    end)

    pcall(function()
        Highlight.Clear()
    end)

    pcall(function()
        Radar.Clear()
    end)

    pcall(function()
        Draw.ClearAll()
    end)

    pcall(function()
        Actions.ResetCamera()
    end)

    pcall(function()
        RestoreBackup()
    end)

    pcall(function()
        SkinChanger.Unload()
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if Effects.Tint then
            Effects.Tint:Destroy()
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        Workspace.Gravity = 196.2
    end)

    pcall(function()
        ScreenGui:Destroy()
    end)

    pcall(function()
        Window:Destroy()
    end)

    _G.RIVALS_WINDOW = nil
    _G.RIVALS_GUI = nil
    _G.RIVALS_STATE = nil
    _G.RIVALS_UTIL = nil
    _G.RIVALS_AIMDAMP = nil
    _G.RIVALS_ESP = nil
    _G.RIVALS_MOVEMENT = nil
    _G.RIVALS_AIM = nil
    _G.RIVALS_SKIN = nil
end

_G.RIVALS_UNLOAD = Unload

-- The registry survives a re-exec because _G does. The next run walks this
-- list before it builds anything, so a reload can never leave a ghost pass
-- draining frames behind the new one.
table.insert(_G.RIVALS_CLEANUPS, Unload)

--==================================================
-- AUTOLOAD
--==================================================

pcall(function()
    Window:LoadAutoload()
end)

-- Autoload restores whatever the last session had armed. Aimlock and trigger
-- are never restored armed: the user enables them every run.
local aimFlag = Aster.Flags and Aster.Flags.AimEnabled
if aimFlag and aimFlag.Set and aimFlag.Value == true then
    pcall(function()
        aimFlag:Set(false)
    end)
end

local triggerFlag = Aster.Flags and Aster.Flags.TriggerEnabled
if triggerFlag and triggerFlag.Set and triggerFlag.Value == true then
    pcall(function()
        triggerFlag:Set(false)
    end)
end

Config.AimEnabled = false
Config.TriggerEnabled = false
State.AimHeld = false
State.AimToggled = false
State.Target = nil
State.TargetPart = nil

--==================================================
-- READY
--==================================================

Toast.Push("RIVALS // SIGNAL", "Loaded. RightShift toggles the menu.", Config.AccentColor)

-- Capability read-out: the three things that silently switch a whole subsystem
-- off are Drawing (every ESP element), mousemoverel (pointer aim) and mouse1click
-- (triggerbot / auto fire). Naming them at load turns "it does nothing" into a
-- one-line answer.
local capabilities = string.format(
    "drawing=%s mouse=%s click=%s camera=%s",
    Draw.Available and "yes" or "no",
    Util.HasMouseMove() and "yes" or "no",
    Util.HasMouseClick() and "yes" or "no",
    tostring(Cam.Get() and Cam.Get().CameraType)
)

Notify(
    "RIVALS // SIGNAL",
    string.format("Loaded on %s | %s", Util.GetExecutor(), capabilities),
    "check",
    4
)

return "RIVALS // SIGNAL 1.4.0 loaded"


]==],

    RivalsMobile = [==[
--[[
    RIVALS // SIGNAL
    VERSION 1.4.0  -  ASTER UI

    A single-file hub for Rivals. Everything below is written against the
    stock Roblox data model: Players, Character, Humanoid, HumanoidRootPart,
    Camera, Tool. No offsets, no per-update signatures, nothing that dies
    when the game pushes a patch.

    AIM GLIDE  (1.3.0)
      Smoothing used to be a fraction of the remaining angle applied once per
      frame, so the same slider value turned four times faster at 240 Hz than
      at 60 Hz and the last degree snapped into place. It is now a convergence
      rate in radians per second, shaped by distance-to-target, with an optional
      acquisition ramp and deterministic micro-movement. The camera fallback
      shares the same curve, so changing Input Method no longer changes how the
      lock feels.

    AIM DELIVERY  (1.2.2)
      Rivals runs CameraType.Scriptable and writes the camera itself every
      frame, so a RenderStepped CFrame write is overwritten before it renders -
      which is why 1.1.0's aimlock looked dead. The default method now steers
      the pointer with mousemoverel and closes the loop on measured screen
      error, so no sensitivity constant is assumed and the game's own camera
      code does the turning. The CFrame path is still there as a fallback and
      is bound at the end of the render step.

    RIVALS SPECIFICS  (checked live, place 17625359962 / universe 6035872082)
      - Every fighter is R15 with MaxHealth 150.
      - No Team objects exist. Side information is a numeric TeamID attribute
        on the player and is absent in free-for-all, so the team filters read
        that attribute and fall back to Team.
      - The game builds its own hitbox rig per character: HitboxHead (2^3,
        IsCritical), HitboxBody (3.5x4x2.5), HitboxHeadSmall, HitboxBodySmall
        (IsSmallHitbox) and PhysicalHitboxHead (the only collidable one). Aim
        and the hitbox expander anchor to those, not to limb MeshParts.
      - Player attributes carry Level, DisplayELO, PlayerStatus, TeamID and
        EnvironmentID, which is where the ESP info line reads from.
      - In the hub a fighter has no Tool and an empty Backpack, so the weapon
        label only resolves mid-match.

    TABS
      Aim       - aimlock, triggerbot, hitbox expander, FOV circle, prediction,
                  smoothing, target highlight, auto fire, entity hitboxes
      ESP       - boxes, names, health, distance, tracers, skeleton, head dots,
                  weapon labels, level/ELO/status line, box fill, off-screen
                  arrows, player highlight, rotating radar
      Visuals   - chams, crosshair designer, core UI hiding, world tint, fog,
                  skybox, time of day, fullbright, camera FOV
      Combat    - hit sounds, hit markers, damage numbers, auto reload,
                  weapon info readout
      Local     - walk speed, jump power, jump height, gravity, fly, noclip,
                  infinite jump, spin, hip height, click teleport, teleport,
                  anti-afk, reset, rejoin, server hop, job id
      Players   - live list, spectate, teleport, copy name, copy user id,
                  camera reset, nearest follow
      Customize - watermark, stats overlay, keybind list, notifications,
                  accent colour, about panel
      Settings  - config manager, theme manager, interface manager,
                  panic switches, unload

    SKIN CHANGER  (1.4.0)
      A SkinChanger tab drives the supplied Aniha cosmetic hooks from inside
      the hub instead of opening a second window. The weapon and wrap lists are
      rebuilt from the live CosmeticLibrary at load, then cached to
      RIVALS/Skins.json. ClientViewModel.new and GetWrap are wrapped, and the
      originals are restored on unload so a reload cannot stack hooks.

    CLEANUP
      Every connection, drawing object, highlight, clone and watcher lives in
      a registry. Reloading the script tears down the previous instance first,
      so no ghost ESP follows you into the next server.
]]

--==================================================
-- SERVICES
--==================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

--==================================================
-- CLEANUP REGISTRY
--==================================================

-- A reload is not a fresh page. Everything a previous instance opened stays
-- open until it is closed, and a second ESP pass on top of the first one is
-- how a hub ends up burning 40% of a frame on duplicated boxes.
if type(_G.RIVALS_CLEANUPS) == "table" then
    for _, cleanup in ipairs(_G.RIVALS_CLEANUPS) do
        pcall(cleanup)
    end
    table.clear(_G.RIVALS_CLEANUPS)
else
    _G.RIVALS_CLEANUPS = {}
end

local Connections = {}
local Destroyed = false

local function Connect(signal, callback)
    local connection = signal:Connect(callback)
    table.insert(Connections, connection)
    return connection
end

local function DisconnectAll()
    for _, connection in ipairs(Connections) do
        pcall(function()
            connection:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    table.clear(Connections)
end

--==================================================
-- AIM MODE OVERRIDES
--==================================================

-- Rage does not copy the Aim tab's values and overwrite them; it substitutes
-- its own at read time. Switching Rage off therefore restores whatever the
-- player had configured, with no save/restore bookkeeping to get wrong.
local function AimCfg(key)
    if Config.RageEnabled then
        local value = Config["Rage" .. key]
        if value ~= nil then
            return value
        end
    end
    return Config["Aim" .. key]
end

-- Rage can run by itself: turning the toggle on is enough, no separate Aimbot
-- master switch. RageRequireKey puts the aim key back in charge.
local function AimActive()
    return Config.AimEnabled or Config.RageEnabled
end
